# Navakanipāta (AN 9)

> **Nikāya:** Aṅguttaranikāya
> **Nipāta:** AN 9 (Navakanipāta)
> **PTS Volume:** Aṅguttara Nikāya Vol. 4
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 311 -->
<a id="page-311"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 311]*

sāvakā bhavissanti . . .[^311-1] yāva me upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacāriniyo sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahetvā ācikkhissanti desissanti[^311-2] paññāpessanti[^311-3] paṭṭhapessanti[^311-4] vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppannaṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā[^311-5] sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desissantī)) ti. Etarahi bhante upāsikā Bhagavato sāvikā viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacāriniyo sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahetvā ācikkhanti desenti paññāpenti paṭṭhapenti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaronti, uppannaṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā[^311-5] sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desenti. Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu Sugato, parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato, bhāsitā kho pan'; esā bhante Bhagavatā vācā ((na tāvāhaṃ pāpima parinibbāyissāmi, yāva me idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na iddhañ c'; eva bhavissati phītañ ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujaññaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva devamanussehi suppakāsitan)) ti. Etarahi bhante Bhagavato brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ c'; eva phītañ ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujaññaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva devamanussehi suppakāsitaṃ. Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu Sugato, parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato'; ti. ‘Appossukko tvaṃ pāpima hohi, na ciraṃ Tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati, ito tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ accayena Tathāgato parinibbāyissatī'; ti.

9. Atha kho Bhagavā Cāpālacetiye sato sampajāno āyusaṅkhāraṃ ossaji[^311-6]. Ossaṭṭhe[^311-7] Bhagavatā āyusaṅkhāre mahābhūmicālo ahosi bhiṃsanako salomahaṃso[^311-8], devadundubhiyo[^311-9] ca phaliṃsu. Atha kho Bhagavā etam atthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ velāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ[^311-10] udānesi[^311-10].


<!-- PTS Page 312 -->
<a id="page-312"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 312]*

Tulam atulañ ca sambhavaṃ bhavasaṅkhāram avassaji[^312-1] muni ajjhattarato samāhito abhindi kavacam iv'; attasambhavan[^312-2] ti.

10. Atha kho āyasmato Ānandassa etad ahosi ‘mahā vatāyaṃ bhūmicālo[^312-3], sumahā vatāyaṃ bhūmicālo bhiṃsanako salomahaṃso, devadundubhiyo ca phaliṃsu; ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā' ti? Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘mahā vatāyaṃ bhante bhūmicālo, sumahā[^312-4] vatāyaṃ[^312-4] bhante[^312-4] bhūmicālo[^312-4] bhiṃsanako salomahaṃso, devadundubhiyo ca phaliṃsu; ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā'; ti?

11. Aṭṭh'; ime Ānanda hetū aṭṭha paccayā mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya. Katame aṭṭha?

12. Yaṃ[^312-5] Ānanda mahāpaṭhavī udake patiṭṭhitā, udakaṃ vāte patiṭṭhitaṃ, vāto ākāsaṭṭho hoti: so Ānanda samayo, yaṃ mahāvātā vāyanti, mahāvātā vāyantā udakaṃ kampenti, udakaṃ kampitaṃ paṭhaviṃ kampeti. Ayaṃ Ānanda paṭhamo hetu paṭhamo paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

13. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā iddhimā cetovasippatto, devatā[^312-6] mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā; tassa parittā paṭhavīsaññā[^312-7] bhāvitā hoti appamāṇā āposaññā: so imaṃ paṭhaviṃ kampeti[^312-8] saṃkampeti[^312-9] sampakampeti[^312-10]. Ayaṃ Ānanda dutiyo hetu dutiyo paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

14. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda yadā bodhisatto Tusitākāya cavitvā sato sampajāno mātukucchiṃ okkamati, tadāyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 313 -->
<a id="page-313"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 313]*

paṭhavī kampati saṃkampati sampakampati[^313-1]. Ayaṃ Ānanda tatiyo hetu tatiyo paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

15. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda yadā bodhisatto sato sampajāno mātukucchismā nikkhamati, tadāyaṃ paṭhavī kampati saṃkampati[^313-2] sampakampati[^313-3]. Ayaṃ Ānanda catuttho hetu catuttho paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

16. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda yadā Tathāgato anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambujjhati, tadāyaṃ paṭhavī kampati saṃkampati[^313-2] sampakampati. Ayaṃ Ānanda pañcamo hetu pañcamo paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

17. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda yadā Tathāgato anuttaraṃ dhammacakkaṃ pavatteti, tadāyaṃ paṭhavī kampati saṃkampati[^313-2] sampakampati[^313-4]. Ayaṃ Ānanda chaṭṭho hetu chaṭṭho paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

18. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda yadā Tathāgato sato sampajāno āyusaṅkhāraṃ ossajati[^313-5], tadāyaṃ paṭhavī kampati saṃkampati[^313-2] sampakampati[^313-4]. Ayaṃ Ānanda sattamo hetu sattamo paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya.

19. Puna ca paraṃ Ānanda yadā Tathāgato anupādisesāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, tadāyaṃ paṭhavī kampati saṃkampati[^313-2] sampakampati[^313-4]. Ayaṃ Ānanda aṭṭhamo hetu aṭṭhamo paccayo mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya. Ime kho Ānanda aṭṭha hetū aṭṭha paccayā mahato bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā ti. Bhūmicālavaggo[^313-6] sattamo[^313-7]. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^313-8]:

<a id="page-314"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 314]*

Icchā alañ ca saṃkhittaṃ Gayā[^314-1] abhibhūnā[^314-2] te[^314-3] saha Vimokho dve ca vohārā parisā[^314-4] bhūmicālenā[^314-5] ti. LXXI[^314-6].

1. Saddho ca[^314-7] bhikkhave bhikkhu hoti, no ca[^314-8] sīlavā. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddho ca assaṃ sīlavā cā'; ti. Yato ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti.

2. Saddho ca bhikkhave bhikkhu hoti sīlavā ca[^314-9], no ca bahussuto. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddho ca assaṃ sīlavā ca bahussuto cā'; ti. Yato ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca bahussuto ca, evaṃ so ten' aṅgena paripūro hoti.

3. Saddho ca[^314-10] bhikkhave bhikkhu hoti sīlavā ca[^314-11] bahussuto ca[^314-11], no ca dhammakathiko . . . pe[^314-12] . . . dhammakathiko ca, no ca parisāvacaro . . .[^314-13] parisāvacaro ca, no ca visārado[^314-14] parisāya[^314-15] dhammaṃ deseti . . .[^314-13] visārado[^314-16] ca parisāya[^314-15] dhammaṃ deseti, no ca catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^314-17] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī . . .[^314-13] catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, no ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Tena taṃ aṅgaṃ

paripūretabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddho ca assaṃ sīlavā ca1 bahussuto ca dhammakathiko[^315-2] ca[^315-2] parisāvacaro[^315-2] ca[^315-2] visārado ca parisāya[^315-3] dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ[^315-4], catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī assaṃ akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ[^315-5] khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti. Yato ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho ca[^315-6] hoti sīlavā ca[^315-6] bahussuto ca dhammakathiko ca parisāvacaro ca visārado ca[^315-7] parisāya dhammaṃ deseti, catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ[^315-5] khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu samantapāsādiko ca hoti sabbākāraparipūro cā ti. LXXII[^315-8].

1. Saddho ca[^315-9] bhikkhave bhikkhu hoti, no ca sīlavā. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddho ca[^315-2] assaṃ sīlavā cā'; ti. Yato ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca, evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti.

2. Saddho ca[^315-2] bhikkhave bhikkhu hoti sīlavā ca, no ca bahussuto . . . pe[^315-10] . . . bahussuto ca, no ca dhammakathiko . . .[^315-11] dhammakathiko ca, no ca parisāvacaro . . .[^315-11] parisāvacaro ca, no ca visārado parisāya dhammaṃ deseti

. . .[^316-1] visārado ca parisāya dhammaṃ deseti, no[^316-2] ca[^316-2] ye[^316-3] te santā vimokhā atikkamma rūpe āruppā[^316-4], te kāyena phusitvā[^316-5] viharati; ye[^316-6] te santā vimokhā atikkamma rūpe āruppā, te kāyena phusitvā[^316-7] viharati, no āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddho ca[^316-8] assaṃ[^316-9] sīlavā ca bahussuto[^316-10] ca dhammakathiko ca parisāvacaro ca visārado ca parisāya[^316-11] dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ; ye[^316-3] te santā vimokhā atikkamma rūpe āruppā, te kāyena phusitvā[^316-12] vihareyyaṃ, āsavānaṃ[^316-13] khayā . . . pe[^316-14] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti. Yato ca kho bhikkhave bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca bahussuto ca dhammakathiko ca parisāvacaro ca visārado ca parisāya[^316-15] dhammaṃ deseti; ye[^316-3] te santā vimokhā atikkamma rūpe āruppā, te kāyena phusitvā[^316-12] viharati, āsavānaṃ[^316-13] khayā . . . pe[^316-16] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu samantapāsādiko ca[^316-17] hoti sabbākāraparipūro cā ti. LXXIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Nāṭike[^316-18] viharati Giñjakāvasathe[^316-19]. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:--


<!-- PTS Page 317 -->
<a id="page-317"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 317]*

Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante[^317-1] ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Maraṇasati bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Bhāvetha no tumhe bhikkhave maraṇasatin ti.

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ahaṃ kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ[^317-2] vata me kataṃ assā>> ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

4. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā>> ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

5. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti ((aho vatāhaṃ upaḍḍhadivasaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā)) ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

6. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti ((aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā)) ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

7. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ahaṃ pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha

mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ upaḍḍhapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā>> ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

8. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattāro pañca ālope saṃkhāditvā[^318-1] ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā>> ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

9. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ ālopaṃ saṃkhāditvā ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ[^318-2] vata me kataṃ assā>> ti: evaṃkho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

10. Aññataro pi kho bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘aham pi kho bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana tvaṃ bhikkhu bhāvesi maraṇasatin'; ti? ‘Idha mayhaṃ bhante evaṃ hoti <<aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi[^318-3] passasitvā vā assasāmi[^318-3], Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ[^318-4] vata me kataṃ assā>> ti: evaṃ kho ahaṃ bhante bhāvemi maraṇasatin'; ti.

11. Evaṃ vutte Bhagavā te bhikkhū etad avoca:-- Yvāyaṃ[^318-5] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Yo pāyaṃ[^318-6] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho


<!-- PTS Page 319 -->
<a id="page-319"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 319]*

vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Yo pāyaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ upaḍḍhadivasaṃ jīveyyaṃ, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Yo pāyaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Yo pāyaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ upaḍḍhapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Yo pāyaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattāro pañca ālope saṃkhāditvā ajjhoharāmi, Bhagayato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave bhikkhū pamattā viharanti, dandhaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāventi āsavānaṃ khayāya. Yo ca[^319-1] khvāyaṃ[^319-2] bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ ekaṃ ālopaṃ saṃkhāditvā ajjhoharāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Yo pāyaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu evaṃ maraṇasatiṃ bhāveti ‘aho vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīveyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi passasitvā vā assasāmi, Bhagavato sāsanaṃ manasikareyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata me kataṃ assā'; ti. Ime vuccanti bhikkhave bhikkhū appamattā viharanti, tikkhaṃ[^319-1] maraṇasatiṃ bhāventi āsavānaṃ khayāya. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ:-- Appamattā viharissāma, tikkhaṃ[^319-3] maraṇasatiṃ bhāvessāma[^319-4] āsavānaṃ khayāyā ti. Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabban ti.


<!-- PTS Page 320 -->
<a id="page-320"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 320]*

LXXIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Nāṭike[^320-1] viharati Giñjakāvasathe[^320-2]. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi . . . pe[^320-3] . . . Maraṇasati bhikkhave bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Kathaṃ bhāvitā ca bhikkhave maraṇasati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu divase nikkhante rattiyā paṭihitāya[^320-4] iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘bahukā kho me paccayā maraṇassa: ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya[^320-5], vicchikā vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya[^320-6], satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃseyya[^320-6], tena me assa kālakiriyā, so mama assa antarāyo; upakkhalitvā[^320-7] vā papateyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā me bhuttaṃ byāpajjeyya, pittaṃ vā me kuppeyya, semhaṃ vā me kuppeyya, satthakā vā me vātā kuppeyyaṃ, manussā vā maṃ upakkameyyuṃ, amanussā vā maṃ upakkameyyuṃ, tena me assa kālakiriyā, so mama assa antarāyo'; ti. Tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā iti paṭisañcikkhitabbaṃ ‘atthi nu kho me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā[^320-8], ye me assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā'; ti. Sace bhikkhave bhikkhu paccavekkhamāno evaṃ jānāti ‘atthi me pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā, ye me assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā'; ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ ca karaṇīyaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ādittacelo vā ādittasīso vā tass'; eva celassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ ca vāyāmañ ca ussāhañ ca ussoḷhiñ ca appaṭivāniñ ca satiñ ca sampajaññañ ca kareyya, evam eva kho bhikkhave tena bhikkhunā tesaṃ yeva pāpakānaṃ

appahīnā, ye me assu divā kālaṃ karontassa antarāyāyā' ti, tena bhikkhave bhikkhunā ten'; eva pītipāmojjena vihātabbaṃ ahorattānusikkhinā kusalesu dhammesu. Evaṃ bhāvitā kho bhikkhave maraṇasati evaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti. LXXV.

1. Aṭṭh'; imā bhikkhave sampadā. Katamā aṭṭha?

2. Uṭṭhānasampadā, ārakkhasampadā, kalyāṇamittatā, samajīvitā[^322-1], saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, paññāsampadā. Imā kho bhikkhave aṭṭha sampadā ti. LXXVI.

1. Aṭṭh'; imā bhikkhave sampadā. Katamā aṭṭha?

2. Uṭṭhānasampadā, ārakkhasampadā, kalyāṇamittatā, samajīvitā[^322-8], saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, paññāsampadā. Katamā ca bhikkhave uṭṭhānasampadā?

3. Idha bhikkhave kulaputto yena kammuṭṭhānenaḍ jīvikaṃ[^322-9] kappeti yadi kasiyā yadi vaṇijjāya[^322-10] yadi gorakkhena yadi issatthena yadi rājaporisena yadi sippaññatarena

4. Idha bhikkhave kulaputtassa bhogā honti uṭṭhānaviriyādhigatā bāhābalaparicitā sedāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā, te[^323-2] ārakkhena guttiyā sampādeti ‘kint'; ime[^323-3] bhoge neva rājāno hareyyuṃ, na corā hareyyuṃ, na aggi ḍaheyya[^323-4], na udakaṃ vaheyya[^323-5], na appiyā dāyādā hareyyun' ti. 5. Idha bhikkhave kulaputto yasmiṃ gāme vā nigame[^323-6] vā[^323-6] paṭivasati, tattha[^323-7] ye te honti gahapatī vā gahapatiputtā[^323-8] vā daharā vā vuddhasīlino vuddhā[^323-9] vā[^323-9] vuddhasīlino[^323-9] saddhāsampannā sīlasampannā[^323-6] cāgasampannā paññāsampannā, tehi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhati[^323-10] sallapati sākacchaṃ samāpajjati; yathārūpānaṃ saddhāsampannānaṃ saddhāsampadaṃ[^323-11] anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ sīlasampannānaṃ sīlasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ cāgasampannānaṃ cāgasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ paññāsampannānaṃ paññāsampadaṃ anusikkhati. 6. Idha bhikkhave kulaputto āyañ[^323-12] ca bhogānaṃ viditvā vayañ[^323-6] ca[^323-6] bhogānaṃ[^323-6] viditvā[^323-6] samaṃ[^323-13] jīvikaṃ kappeti na[^323-14] accogāḷhaṃ[^323-14] na[^323-15] atihīnaṃ[^323-15] ‘evaṃ me āyo[^323-16] vayaṃ[^323-16] pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca me vayo āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī'; ti. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave tulādhāro vā tulādhārantevāsī vā tulaṃ paggahetvā jānāti ‘ettakena[^323-17] vā[^323-17] onataṃ[^323-18] etta-

Sacāyaṃ bhikkhave kulaputto appāyo samāno uḷāraṃ jīvikaṃ kappeti, tassa bhavanti vattāro ‘udumbarakhādikaṃ[^324-5]'; vāyaṃ[^324-6] kulaputto bhoge khādatī'; ti. Idhāvuso bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ[^329-9] ca[^329-10] dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, no ca dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, no ca attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti, kalyāṇavāco[^329-11] ca[^329-10] hoti[^329-12] . . . pe[^329-6] . . . sandassako ca[^329-10] hoti[^329-13] . . .[^329-14] sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho āvuso catūhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^329-15] attano.

9. Tīh'[^329-16] āvuso dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^329-17] paresaṃ. Katamehi tīhi?

10. Idhāvuso bhikkhu na h'; eva kho[^329-13] khippanisanti ca[^329-18] hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhāra-

kajātiko[^330-1] hoti, dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, attham[^330-2] aññāya dhammam[^330-3] aññāya[^330-3] dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^330-4] hoti, no ca kalyāṇavāco hoti . . . pe[^330-5] . . . no ca sandassako hoti . . .[^330-6] sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho āvuso tīhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^330-7] paresaṃ.

11. Tīh'; āvuso dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^330-7] attano. Katamehi tīhi?

12. Idhāvuso bhikkhu na h'; eva kho khippanisanti ca[^330-8] hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, no ca dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, no ca attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti, kalyāṇavāco[^330-9] ca[^330-4] hoti . . . pe[^330-10] . . . sandassako[^330-11] ca[^330-12] hoti . . . pe[^330-13] . . . sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho āvuso tīhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^330-7] attano.

13. Dvīh'; āvuso dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^330-7] paresaṃ. Katamehi dvīhi?

14. Idhāvuso bhikkhu na h'; eva kho khippanisanti ca[^330-8] hoti kusalesu dhammesu, sutānañ[^330-14] ca[^330-15] dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^330-4] hoti, no ca kalyāṇavāco hoti . . . pe[^330-5] . . . no ca sandassako hoti . . . pe[^330-5] . . . sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho āvuso dvīhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ attano no[^330-16] paresaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 331 -->
<a id="page-331"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 331]*

15. Dvīh'; āvuso dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^331-1] attano. Katamehi dvīhi?

16. Idhāvuso bhikkhu na h'; eva kho khippanisanti ca[^331-2] hoti kusalesu dhammesu, no ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātiko hoti, no[^331-3] ca dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthupaparikkhī hoti, no ca attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti, kalyāṇavāco ca[^331-4] hoti kalyāṇavākkaraṇo[^331-5] poriyā vācāya samannāgato vissaṭṭhāya[^331-6] anelagaḷāya atthassa viññāpaniyā, sandassako ca hoti samādapako samuttejako sampahaṃsako sabrahmacārīnaṃ. Imehi kho āvuso dvīhi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu alaṃ paresaṃ no[^331-7] attano ti. LXXIX.

1. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave dhammā sekhassa[^331-8] bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyesu aguttadvāratā, bhojane amattaññutā, saṃsaggārāmatā, papañcārāmatā. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno parihānāya saṃvattanti.

3. Aṭṭh'; ime bhikkhave dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattanti. Katame aṭṭha?

4. Na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyesu guttadvāratā, bhojane mattaññutā, asaṃsaggārāmatā, nippapañcārāmatā. Ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha dhammā sekhassa bhikkhuno aparihānāya saṃvattantī ti[^331-9].


<!-- PTS Page 332 -->
<a id="page-332"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 332]*

LXXX.

1. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave kusītavatthūni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

2. Idha bhikkhave[^332-1] bhikkhunā kammaṃ kattabbaṃ[^332-2] hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘kammaṃ kho[^332-3] me kattabbaṃ bhavissati, kammaṃ kho pana me karontassa kāyo kilamissati, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī[^332-4]'; ti. So nipajjati na viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamaṃ kusītavatthuṃ[^332-5].

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho kammaṃ akāsiṃ[^332-6], kammaṃ kho pana me karontassa kāyo kilanto, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So nipajjati na viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave dutiyaṃ kusītavatthuṃ.

4. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā maggo gantabbo[^332-7] hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘maggo kho me gantabbo[^332-7] bhavissati, maggaṃ kho pana me gacchantassa kāyo kilamissati, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So nipajjati na viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave tatiyaṃ kusītavatthuṃ.

5. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā maggo gato hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho maggaṃ agamāsiṃ[^332-8], maggaṃ kho pana me gacchantassa[^332-9] kāyo kilanto, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So nipajjati na viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave catutthaṃ kusītavatthuṃ.

6. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ[^332-10]. Tassa evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 333 -->
<a id="page-333"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 333]*

hoti ‘ahaṃ kho gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto nālatthaṃ[^333-1] lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa me kāyo kilanto akammañño, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So nipajjati[^333-2] . . . pe[^333-3] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave pañcamaṃ kusītavatthuṃ.

7. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto labhati[^333-4] lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto alatthaṃ[^333-5] lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa me kāyo garuko akammañño māsācitaṃ[^333-6] maññe, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So[^333-7] nipajjati[^333-7] . . . pe[^333-8] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭhaṃ kusītavatthuṃ.

8. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno[^333-9] uppanno hoti appamattako ābādho. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘uppanno kho me ayaṃ appamattako ābādho, atthi kappo nipajjituṃ, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So nipajjati[^333-10] . . . pe[^333-11] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave sattamaṃ kusītavatthuṃ.

9. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhito hoti aciravuṭṭhito gelaññā. Tassa[^333-12] evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho gilānā vuṭṭhito aciravuṭṭhito gelaññā, tassa me kāyo dubbalo akammañño, handāhaṃ nipajjāmī'; ti. So nipajjati na viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamaṃ kusītavatthuṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha kusītavatthūnī ti[^333-13].


<!-- PTS Page 334 -->
<a id="page-334"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 334]*

10. Aṭṭh'; imāni bhikkhave ārabbhavatthūni. Katamāni aṭṭha?

11. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhunā kammaṃ kattabbaṃ hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘kammaṃ kho me kattabbaṃ bhavissati, kammaṃ kho pana[^334-1] me[^334-2] karontena na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ paṭigacc'[^334-3] eva viriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā'; ti. So viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave paṭhamaṃ[^334-4] ārabbhavatthuṃ.

12. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hoti. Tassa[^334-5] evaṃ[^334-5] hoti[^334-5] ‘ahaṃ kho kammaṃ akāsiṃ[^334-6], kammaṃ kho panāhaṃ karonto[^334-7] nāsakkhiṃ[^334-8] buddhānaṃ[^334-9] sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ viriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā'; ti. So viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa[^334-10] pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave dutiyaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ.

13. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā maggo gantabbo[^334-11] hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘maggo kho me gantabbo[^334-11] bhavissati, maggaṃ kho pana me[^334-12] gacchantena na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ paṭigacc' eva[^334-13] viriyaṃ ārabhāmi . . .[^334-14] Idaṃ bhikkhave tatiyaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ.

14. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhunā maggo gato hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho maggaṃ agamāsiṃ[^334-15], maggaṃ kho panāhaṃ gacchanto nāsakkhiṃ[^334-16] buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasikātuṃ, handāhaṃ viriyaṃ ārabhāmi . . . pe[^334-14] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave catutthaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 335 -->
<a id="page-335"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 335]*

15. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto nālatthaṃ[^335-1] lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa me kāyo lahuko kammañño, handāhaṃ viriyaṃ ārabhāmi . . . pe[^335-2] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave pañcamaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ.

16. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ, tassa me kāyo balavā kammañño, handāhaṃ viriyaṃ ārabhāmi . . . pe[^335-2] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave chaṭṭhaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ.

17. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno uppanno hoti appamattako ābādho. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘uppanno kho me ayaṃ appamattako ābādho, ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ[^335-3] me ābādho pavaḍḍheyya, handāhaṃ paṭigacc'; eva viriyaṃ ārabhāmi . . . pe[^335-2] . . . Idaṃ bhikkhave sattamaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ.

18. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhito hoti aciravuṭṭhito gelaññā. Tassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho gilānā vuṭṭhito aciravuṭṭhito gelaññā, ṭhānaṃ kho pan' etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me ābādho paccudāvatteyya, handāhaṃ paṭigacc'; eva viriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā'; ti. So viriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ bhikkhave aṭṭhamaṃ ārabbhavatthuṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave aṭṭha ārabbhavatthūnī ti. Yamakavaggo aṭṭhamo[^335-4]. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^335-5]:


<!-- PTS Page 336 -->
<a id="page-336"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 336]*

Dve[^336-1] saddhā[^336-1] dve maraṇasati[^336-2] dve[^336-3] sampadā[^336-3] athāpare[^336-4] Icchā alaṃ[^336-5] parihānaṃ kusītaṃ[^336-6] ārabbhavatthunī ti. LXXXI[^336-7].

1. Satisampajaññe bhikkhave asati satisampajaññavipannassa[^336-8] hatupanisaṃ[^336-9] hoti hirottappaṃ, hirottappe asati hirottappavipannassa hatupaniso hoti indriyasaṃvaro, indriyasaṃvare asati indriyasaṃvaravipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti sīlaṃ, sīle asati sīlavipannassa hatupaniso hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipannassa hatupaniso hoti nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge asati nibbidāvirāgavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsavipanno, tassa papaṭikā[^336-10] pi[^336-11] na pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave satisampajaññe asati satisampajaññavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti hirottappaṃ, hirottappe asati hirottappavipannassa hatupanisaṃ hoti . . . pe[^336-12] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ.

2. Satisampajaññe bhikkhave sati satisampajaññasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ[^336-13] hoti hirottappaṃ, hirottappe sati hirottappasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti indriyasaṃvaro, indriyasaṃvare sati indriyasaṃvarasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti sīlaṃ, sīle sati sīlasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti sammāsamādhi, sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassane sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampannassa upanisasampanno hoti


<!-- PTS Page 337 -->
<a id="page-337"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 337]*

nibbidāvirāgo, nibbidāvirāge sati nibbidāvirāgasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave rukkho sākhāpalāsasampanno, tassa papaṭikā pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, taco pi pheggu pi sāro pi pāripūriṃ gacchati, evam eva kho bhikkhave satisampajaññe satisampajaññasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti hirottappaṃ, hirottappe sati hirottappasampannassa upanisasampannaṃ hoti . . . pe[^337-1] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanan ti. LXXXII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Puṇṇiyo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā . . . pe[^337-2] . . . Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Puṇṇiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ko nu kho bhante hetu ko paccayo, yena app ekadā Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti, app ekadā na paṭibhātī'; ti?

2. Saddho ca[^337-3] Puṇṇiya bhikkhu hoti, no ca[^337-4] upasaṅkamitā[^337-5], neva[^337-6] tāva[^337-7] Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti. Yato ca kho Puṇṇiya bhikkhu saddho ca hoti upasaṅkamitā ca[^337-8], evaṃ[^337-9] Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti[^337-10]. Saddho ca Puṇṇiya bhikkhu hoti upasaṅkamitā ca, no ca[^337-3] payirupāsitā . . . pe[^337-1] . . . Payirupāsitā ca, no ca[^337-3] paripucchitā . . . Paripucchitā ca, no ca[^337-3] ohitasoto[^337-11] dhammaṃ suṇāti . . . Ohitasoto[^337-12] ca[^337-12] dhammaṃ[^337-12] suṇāti[^337-12], no ca[^337-3] sutvā dhammaṃ[^337-3] dhāreti . . . Sutvā[^337-13] ca[^337-13] dhammaṃ[^337-13] dhāreti[^337-13], no ca[^337-3] dhatānaṃ[^337-14] dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati . . . Dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ[^337-13] upaparikkhati, no ca[^337-3] attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno hoti, neva[^337-15] tāva[^337-13] Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti. Yato ca kho Puṇṇiya bhikkhu saddho


<!-- PTS Page 338 -->
<a id="page-338"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 338]*

ca hoti[^338-1] upasaṅkamitā ca payirupāsitā ca paripucchitā ca ohitasoto ca dhammaṃ suṇāti sutvā ca[^338-2] dhammaṃ dhāreti dhatānañ ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipanno ca[^338-3] hoti, evaṃ Tathāgataṃ dhammadesanā paṭibhāti. Imehi kho Puṇṇiya[^338-4] dhammehi samannāgato[^338-5] ekantapaṭibhānaṃ[^338-6] Tathāgataṃ[^338-7] dhammadesanā hotī ti[^338-8]. LXXXIII.

1. Sace bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā evaṃ puccheyyuṃ ‘kiṃmūlakā āvuso sabbe[^338-9] dhammā, kiṃsambhavā sabbe dhammā, kiṃsamudayā sabbe dhammā, kiṃsamosaraṇā sabbe dhammā, kiṃ-pamukhā[^338-10] sabbe dhammā, kiṃ-ādhipateyyā[^338-11] sabbe dhammā, kiṃ-uttarā sabbe dhammā, kiṃsārā sabbe dhammā'; ti: evaṃ puṭṭhā tumhe bhikkhave tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ kinti vyākareyyāthā ti?

2. Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā Bhagavaṃnettikā Bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā, sādhu bhante Bhagavantaṃ yeva paṭibhātu etassa bhāsitassa attho, Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī ti. Tena hi bhikkhave[^338-12] suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. Evaṃ bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

3. Sace bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā evaṃ puccheyyuṃ ‘kiṃmūlakā āvuso sabbe dhammā, kiṃsambhavā sabbe dhammā, kiṃsamudayā sabbe dhammā, kiṃsamosaraṇā sabbe dhammā, kiṃ-pamukhā sabbe dhammā, kiṃ-


<!-- PTS Page 339 -->
<a id="page-339"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 339]*

ādhipateyyā sabbe dhammā, kiṃ-uttarā sabbe dhammā, kiṃsārā sabbe dhammā'; ti: evaṃ puṭṭhā tumhe bhikkhave tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ evaṃ vyākareyyātha ‘chandamūlakā āvuso sabbe dhammā, manasikārasambhavā sabbe dhammā, phassasamudayā[^339-1] sabbe dhammā, vedanāsamosaraṇā sabbe dhammā, samādhipamukhā sabbe dhammā, satādhipateyyā sabbe dhammā, paññuttarā sabbe dhammā, vimuttisārā sabbe dhammā'; ti: evaṃ puṭṭhā tumhe bhikkhave tesaṃ[^339-2] aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ evaṃ vyākareyyāthā ti. LXXXIV.

1. Aṭṭhahi[^339-3] bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgato mahācoro khippaṃ pariyāpajjati na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Appaharantassa paharati, anavasesaṃ ādiyati[^339-4], itthiṃ hanati[^339-5], kumāriṃ dūseti[^339-6], pabbajitaṃ vilumpati, rājakosaṃ[^339-7] vilumpati[^339-7], accāsanne kammaṃ karoti, na ca nidhānakusalo hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhah'[^339-8] aṅgehi samannāgato mahācoro khippaṃ pariyāpajjati na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti.

3. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgato mahācoro na khippaṃ pariyāpajjati ciraṭṭhitiko hoti. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

4. Na appaharantassa paharati, na anavasesaṃ ādiyati, na itthiṃ hanati[^339-9], na kumāriṃ dūseti, na pabbajitaṃ vilumpati, na rājakosaṃ vilumpati, na accāsanne kammaṃ karoti, nidhānakusalo[^339-10] hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhah'[^339-11] aṅgehi samannāgato mahācoro na khippaṃ pariyāpajjati ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti.

##### *[PTS Page 340]*

LXXXV.

1. ‘Samaṇo'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, ‘brāhmaṇo'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, ‘vedagū'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato[^340-1] sammāsambuddhassa[^340-1], ‘bhisakko'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, ‘nimmalo'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, ‘vimalo'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, ‘ñāṇī'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, ‘vimutto'; ti bhikkhave Tathāgatass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassā ti. Yaṃ[^340-2] samaṇena pattabbaṃ[^340-3] brāhmaṇena vusīmatā yaṃ vedagunā pattabbaṃ[^340-4] bhisakkena anuttaraṃ yaṃ nimmalena pattabbaṃ[^340-5] vimalena sucimatā[^340-6] yaṃ ñāṇinā[^340-7] pattabbaṃ[^340-8] vimuttena anuttaraṃ, so'; haṃ vijitasaṃgāmo mutto mocemi bandhanā nāgo 'mhi paramaṃ[^340-9] danto[^340-9] asekho parinibbuto ti. LXXXVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Kosalesu[^340-10] cārikaṃ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ yena Icchānaṅgalaṃ[^340-11] nāma Kosalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmo tad avasari. Tatra sudaṃ Bhagavā Icchānaṅgale viharati Icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍe.

2. Assosuṃ kho Icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā ‘samaṇo khalu bho Gotamo Sakyaputto Sakyakulā pabbajito Icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppatto Icchānaṅgale viharati Icchā-


<!-- PTS Page 341 -->
<a id="page-341"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 341]*

naṅgalavanasaṇḍe. Taṃ kho pana bhavantaṃ[^341-1] Gotamaṃ evaṃ kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggato ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho . . . pe[^341-2] . . . sādhu kho pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hotī'; ti. Atha kho Icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā[^341-3] tassā rattiyā accayena pahūtaṃ[^341-4] khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyaṃ[^341-5] ādāya yena Icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍo ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake aṭṭhaṃsu uccāsaddā mahāsaddā.

3. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Nāgito[^341-6] Bhagavato upaṭṭhāko hoti. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Nāgitaṃ āmantesi:-- Ke pana te Nāgita uccāsaddā mahāsaddā, kevaṭṭā maññe macche[^341-7] vilope'[^341-8] ti? ‘Ete bhante Icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā pahūtaṃ khādaniyaṃ bhojaniyaṃ ādāya bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhitā Bhagavantaṃ[^341-9] yeva[^341-9] uddissa bhikkhusaṅghañ cā'; ti. Māhaṃ[^341-10] Nāgita yasena samāgamaṃ[^341-11] mā ca mayā yaso; yo[^341-12] kho[^341-13] Nāgita na yimassa nekkhammasukhassa[^341-14] pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa[^341-15] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ[^341-16] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa[^341-17] upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ[^341-18] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, so taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ[^341-5] lābhasakkārasilokasukhaṃ sādiyeyyā ti. ‘Adhivāsetu dāni bhante Bhagavā adhivāsetu Sugato, adhivāsanakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato, yena yen'; eva[^341-19] dāni bhante Bhagavā gamissati, tanninnā'; va[^341-20] bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā negamā c'; eva jānapadā[^341-21] ca. Seyyathā


<!-- PTS Page 342 -->

##### *[PTS Page 342]*

pi bhante thullaphusitake deve vassante yathāninnaṃ udakāni pavattanti[^342-1], evam eva kho bhante yena yen'; eva[^342-2] dāni Bhagavā gamissati, tanninnā 'va[^342-3] bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā negamā c'; eva jānapadā[^342-4] ca. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi bhante Bhagavato sīlapaññāṇan'; ti. Māhaṃ[^342-5] Nāgita yasena samāgamaṃ[^342-6] mā ca mayā yaso; yo[^342-7] kho Nāgita na[^342-8] yimassa[^342-8] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa[^342-9] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassāhaṃ[^342-10] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ[^342-11] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, so taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasakkārasilokasukhaṃ sādiyeyya[^342-12]. Devatā pi kho Nāgita ekaccā na yimassa[^342-13] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhiniyo assu[^342-14] akicchalābhiniyo akasiralābhiniyo, yassāhaṃ[^342-15] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ[^342-16] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Tumhākam pi[^342-17] kho[^342-18] Nāgita saṃgamma samāgamma saṅgaṇikavihāraṃ anuyuttānaṃ viharataṃ evaṃ hoti: na ha[^342-19] nūna[^342-20] 'me[^342-20] āyasmanto imassa nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhino assu[^342-14] akicchalābhino akasiralābhino, yassāhaṃ[^342-15] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ[^342-16] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; tathā hi[^342-21] 'me

āyasmanto saṃgamma samāgamma saṅgaṇikavihāraṃ anuyuttā viharanti.

Tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita evaṃ hoti: na ha[^343-3] nūna[^343-4] 'me[^343-4] āyasmanto imassa nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhino assu[^343-5] akicchalābhino akasiralābhino, yassāhaṃ[^343-6] nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ[^343-7] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; tathā hi[^343-8] 'me āyasmanto aññamaññaṃ[^343-9] aṅgulipatodakehi[^343-10] sañjagghanti saṃkīḷanti.

5. Idhāhaṃ[^343-11] Nāgita bhikkhū[^343-12] passāmi yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ phassasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyutte viharante. Tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita evaṃ hoti: na ha[^343-13] nūna[^343-4] 'me[^343-4] āyasmanto imassa nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhino assu[^343-5] akicchalābhino akasiralābhino, yassāhaṃ nekkhammasukhassa pavivekasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambodhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assaṃ[^343-7] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; tathā hi[^343-14] 'me āyasmanto yavadatthaṃ udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ phassasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttā viharanti.

6. Idhāhaṃ Nāgita bhikkhuṃ[^343-15] passāmi gāmantavihāriṃ[^343-16] samāhitaṃ nisinnaṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ Nāgita evaṃ hoti ‘idāni imaṃ[^343-17] āyasmantaṃ ārāmiko vā saccessati[^343-18] samaṇuddeso vā, taṃ[^343-19] tamhā samādhimhā cāvissatī[^343-20]'; ti.

LX XXVII.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamāno saṅgho pattaṃ nikkujjeyya. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?


<!-- PTS Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 345]*

2. Bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisakkati, bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisakkati, bhikkhūnaṃ anāvāsāya[^345-1] parisakkati, bhikkhūnaṃ[^345-2] akkosati paribhāsati, bhikkhū bhikkhūhi vibhedeti[^345-3], buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhah'[^345-4] aṅgehi samannāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamāno saṅgho pattaṃ nikkujjeyya.

3. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamāno saṅgho pattaṃ ukkujjeyya. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

4. Na bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisakkati, na bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisakkati, na bhikkhūnaṃ anāvāsāya[^345-5] parisakkati, na bhikkhūnaṃ[^345-2] akkosati paribhāsati, na bhikkhū[^345-6] bhikkhūhi vibhedeti[^345-3], buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭh'; aṅgehi samannāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamāno saṅgho pattaṃ ukkujjeyyā ti. LXXXVIII.

1. Aṭṭhahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatassa bhikkhuno ākaṅkhamānā upāsakā appasādaṃ[^345-7] pavedeyyuṃ. Katamehi aṭṭhahi?

2. Gihīnaṃ[^345-8] alābhāya[^345-8] parisakkati[^345-8], gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisakkati, gihīnaṃ[^345-9] akkosati paribhāsati, gihī[^345-10] gihīhi vibhedeti[^345-11], buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, agocare[^345-12] ca[^345-13] naṃ passanti. Imehi kho bhikkhave aṭṭhahi dhammehi samannāgatassa bhikkhuno ākaṅkhamānā upāsakā appasādaṃ pavedeyyuṃ[^345-14].

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya aṭṭha dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame aṭṭha?

2. Rūpī[^349-7] rūpāni passati, ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī bahiddhā rūpāni passati, subhan t'; eva adhimutto hoti, sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime aṭṭha dhammā bhāvetabbā.

3. Rāgassa bhikkhave pariññāya . . . parikkhayāya . . . pahānāya . . . khayāya . . . vayāya . . . virāgāya . . . nirodhāya . . . cāgāya . . . paṭinissaggāya ime aṭṭha dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^349-8].

1. Dosassa . . . mohassa . . . kodhassa . . . upanāhassa . . . makkhassa . . . palāsassa[^349-9] . . . issāya[^349-10] . . . maccha-

riyassa . . . māyāya . . . sāṭheyyassa[^350-1] . . . thambhassa . . . sārambhassa . . . mānassa[^350-2] . . . atimānassa . . . madassa . . . pamādassa[^350-2] abhiññāya . . . pariññāya . . . parikkhayāya . . . pahānāya . . . khayāya . . . vayāya . . . virāgāya . . . nirodhāya . . . cāgāya . . . paṭinissaggāya ime aṭṭha dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. Aṭṭhakanipātaṃ[^350-3] samattaṃ[^350-4].


<!-- PTS Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 351]*

NAVAKA-NIPĀTA. Namo[^351-1] Tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa. I [^351-2].

1. Evam me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi[^351-3]:-- Bhikkhavo ti. Bhadante ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Sace bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā evaṃ puccheyyuṃ: sambodhapakkhikānaṃ[^351-4] āvuso dhammānam kā upanisā bhāvanāyā ti? Evaṃ puṭṭhā tumhe bhikkhave tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ kinti vyākareyyāthā ti? ‘Bhagavaṃmūlakā[^351-5] no bhante dhammā Bhagavaṃnettikā[^351-6] Bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā, sādhu vata bhante Bhagavantaṃ ñeva paṭibhātu etassa bhāsitassa attho, Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī'; ti. Tena hi bhikkhave suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evam bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

3. Sace bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā evaṃ puccheyyuṃ: sambodhapakkhikānaṃ āvuso dhammānaṃ kā upanisā bhāvanāyā ti? Evaṃ puṭṭhā tumhe bhikkhave tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ evaṃ vyākareyyāthā: idhāvuso bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo


<!-- PTS Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 352]*

kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Sambodhapakkhikānaṃ āvuso dhammānaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamā upanisā bhāvanāya.

4. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu[^352-1] vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Sambodhapakkhikānaṃ āvuso dhammānaṃ ayaṃ dutiyā upanisā bhāvanāya.

5. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu yāyaṃ kathā abhisallekhikā cetovivaraṇasappāyā, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivekakathā asaṃsaggakathā viriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā paññākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, evarūpiyā[^352-2] kathāya[^352-3] nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Sambodhapakkhikānaṃ āvuso dhammānaṃ ayaṃ tatiyā upanisā bhāvanāya.

6. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Sambodhapakkhikānaṃ āvuso dhammānaṃ ayaṃ catutthā upanisā bhāvanāya.

7. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu paññavā hoti[^352-3], udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Sambodhapakkhikānaṃ āvuso dhammānaṃ ayaṃ pañcamī upanisā bhāvanāya.

8. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^352-4] kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: sīlavā bhavissati, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharissati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhissati sikkhāpadesu. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: yāyaṃ[^352-5] kathā abhisallekhikā cetovivaraṇasappāyā, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathā . . . pe[^352-6] . . . evarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^352-7] kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: āraddhaviriyo viha-


<!-- PTS Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 353]*

rissati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: paññavā bhavissati, udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Tena ca pana bhikkhave bhikkhunā imesu pañcasu dhammesu patiṭṭhāya cattāro dhammā uttariṃ[^353-1] bhāvetabbā: asubhā bhāvetabbā rāgassa pahānāya, mettā bhāvetabbā vyāpādassa pahānāya, ānāpānasati[^353-2] bhāvetabbā vitakkūpacchedāya, aniccasaññā bhāvetabbā asmimānasamugghātāya. Aniccasaññino bhikkhave bhikkhuno anattasaññā saṇṭhāti, anattasaññī[^353-3] asmimānasamugghātaṃ[^353-4] pāpuṇāti diṭṭh'; eva dhamme nibbānan ti. II.

1. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā[^353-5] ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘nissayasampanno[^353-6] nissayasampanno ti bhante vuccati; kittāvatā nu kho bhante bhikkhu nissayasampanno hotī'; ti?

2. Saddhañ ce bhikkhu nissāya akusalaṃ pajahati kusalaṃ bhāveti, pahīnam ev'; assa[^353-7] taṃ akusalaṃ hoti. Hiriñ ce bhikkhu nissāya . . . pe[^353-8] . . . Ottappañ ce bhikkhu nissāya . . .[^353-9] Viriyañ ce bhikkhu nissāya[^353-10] . . .[^353-11] Paññañ[^353-12] ce bhikkhu nissāya[^353-10] akusalaṃ pajahati kusalaṃ bhāveti, pahīnam ev'; assa taṃ akusalaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 354]*

hoti. Taṃ hi 'ssa bhikkhuno[^354-1] akusalaṃ pahīnaṃ hoti[^354-2] suppahīnaṃ[^354-2], yaṃsa[^354-3] ariyāya[^354-4] paññāya[^354-5] disvā[^354-6] pahīnaṃ. Tena ca pana bhikkhu[^354-7] bhikkhunā imesu pañcasu dhammesu patiṭṭhāya cattāro dhammā[^354-8] upanissāya vihātabbā[^354-9]. Katame cattāro?

3. Idha bhikkhu[^354-7] bhikkhu saṃkhāy'; ekaṃ[^354-2] paṭisevati[^354-2], saṃkhāy'; ekaṃ adhivāseti, saṃkhāy'; ekaṃ parivajjeti[^354-10], saṃkhāy'; ekaṃ vinodeti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhu[^354-7] bhikkhu nissayasampanno hotī ti. III.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Cālikāyaṃ viharati Cālikāpabbate[^354-11]. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Meghiyo Bhagavato upaṭṭhāko hoti. Atha[^354-2] kho[^354-2] āyasmā Meghiyo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhito kho[^354-12] āyasmā Meghiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘icchām' ahaṃ bhante Jantugāmaṃ[^354-13] piṇḍāya pavisitun'; ti. ‘Yassa dāni tvaṃ Meghiya kālaṃ maññasī'; ti.

2. Atha kho āyasmā Meghiyo pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi, Jantugāme piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Kimikālāya[^354-14] nadiyā tīraṃ ten'; upasaṅkami. Addasā[^354-15] kho āyasmā Meghiyo Kimikālāya nadiyā tīre jaṅghāvihāraṃ[^354-16] anucaṅkamamāno[^354-17] anuvicaramāno[^354-18]


<!-- PTS Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 355]*

ambavanaṃ pāsādikaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ. Disvān'; assa etad ahosi ‘pāsādikaṃ vat'; idaṃ ambavanaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, alaṃ vat'; idaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa[^355-1] padhānāya[^355-2], sace maṃ Bhagavā anujāneyya, āgaccheyyāhaṃ imaṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā'[^355-3] ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Meghiyo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Meghiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘idhāhaṃ bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ[^355-4], Jantugāme piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Kimikālāya nadiyā tīraṃ ten'; upasaṅkamiṃ[^355-5], addasaṃ[^355-6] kho ahaṃ[^355-7] bhante Kimikālāya nadiyā tīre jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamāno anuvicaramāno ambavanaṃ pāsādikaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ; disvāna me etad ahosi <<pāsādikam vat'; idaṃ ambavanaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, alaṃ vat' idaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāya, sace maṃ Bhagavā anujāneyya, āgaccheyyāhaṃ[^355-8] imaṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā>> ti; sace[^355-9] maṃ Bhagavā anujāneyya, gaccheyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā'; ti[^355-10]. ‘Āgamehi tāva Meghiya, ekak'; amhā[^355-11] tāva[^355-12] yāva añño pi koci bhikkhu dissatū'; ti.

4. Dutiyam pi kho āyasmā Meghiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘Bhagavato bhante natthi kiñci uttariṃ[^355-13] karaṇīyaṃ, natthi katassa paṭicayo, mayhaṃ kho pana bhante atthi uttariṃ[^355-13] karaṇīyaṃ, atthi katassa paṭicayo; sace maṃ Bhagavā anujāneyya, gaccheyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā'; ti. ‘Āgamehi tāva Meghiya, ekak'; amhā[^355-11] tāva[^355-12] yāva añño pi koci bhikkhu dissatū'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 356]*

5. Tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Meghiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘Bhagavato bhante natthi kiñci uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ, natthi katassa paṭicayo, mayhaṃ kho pana bhante atthi uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ, atthi katassa paṭicayo; sace maṃ Bhagavā anujāneyya, gaccheyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā'; ti. ‘Padhānan ti kho Meghiya vadamānaṃ kinti vadeyyāma? Yassa dāni tvaṃ Meghiya kālaṃ maññasī'; ti.

6. Atha kho āyasmā Meghiyo uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yena taṃ ambavanaṃ ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ[^356-1] ambavanaṃ ajjhogāhetvā[^356-2] aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle divāvihāraṃ nisīdi. Atha kho āyasmato Meghiyassa tasmiṃ ambavane viharantassa yebhuyyena tayo pāpakā akusalā[^356-3] vitakkā[^356-3] samudācaranti, seyyathīdaṃ kāmavitakko vyāpādavitakko vihiṃsāvitakko. Atha kho āyasmato Meghiyassa etad ahosi ‘acchariyaṃ vata bho abbhutaṃ vata bho, saddhāya 'va[^356-4] tañ c'; amhi[^356-5] agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito[^356-6], atha ca pan'; imehi[^356-7] tīhi pāpakehi akusalehi vitakkehi anvāsatto[^356-8]: kāmavitakkena[^356-9] vyāpādavitakkena[^356-9] vihiṃsāvitakkenā'; ti.

7. Atha kho āyasmā Meghiyo yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Meghiyo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: idha mayhaṃ bhante tasmiṃ ambavane viharantassa yebhuyyena tayo pāpakā akusalā[^356-10] vitakkā samudācaranti, seyyathīdaṃ kāmavitakko vyāpādavitakko vihiṃsāvitakko; tassa mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi ‘acchariyaṃ vata bho abbhutaṃ vata bho, saddhāya 'va[^356-11] tañ c'; amhi[^356-12] agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito[^356-6], atha ca[^356-13]


<!-- PTS Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 357]*

pan'; imehi tīhi pāpakehi akusalehi[^357-1] vitakkehi[^357-1] anvāsatto[^357-2]: kāmavitakkena vyāpādavitakkena vihiṃsāvitakkenā'; ti. Aparipakkāya Meghiya cetovimuttiyā pañca dhammā paripakkāya[^357-3] saṃvattanti. Katame pañca?

8. Idha Meghiya bhikkhu kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko. Aparipakkāya Meghiya cetovimuttiyā ayaṃ paṭhamo dhammo paripakkāya saṃvattati.

9. Puna ca paraṃ Meghiya bhikkhu sīlavā hoti[^357-4], pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Aparipakkāya Meghiya cetovimuttiyā ayaṃ dutiyo dhammo paripakkāya saṃvattati.

10. Puna ca paraṃ Meghiya bhikkhu yāyaṃ kathā abhisallekhikā cetovivaraṇasappāyā, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathā[^357-5] santuṭṭhikathā pavivekakathā asaṃsaggakathā viriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā paññākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, evarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī hoti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Aparipakkāya Meghiya cetovimuttiyā ayaṃ tatiyo dhammo paripakkāya saṃvattati.

11. Puna ca paraṃ Meghiya bhikkhu āraddhaviriyo viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya[^357-6], kusalānaṃ[^357-6] dhammānaṃ[^357-6] upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Aparipakkāya Meghiya cetovimuttiyā ayaṃ catuttho dhammo paripakkāya saṃvattati.

12. Puna ca paraṃ Meghiya bhikkhu paññavā hoti, udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Aparipakkāya Meghiya cetovimuttiyā ayaṃ pañcamo dhammo paripakkāya saṃvattati.

13. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ Meghiya bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^357-7] kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: sīlavā bhavissati, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto[^357-8] viharissati ācāragocara-

sampanno, anumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhissati sikkhāpadesu. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ Meghiya bhikkhuno pātikaṅkhaṃ[^358-1] kalyāṇasahāyassa {kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa}: yāyaṃ kathā abhisallekhikā cetovivaraṇasappāyā, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathā . . . pe[^358-2] . . . vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā[^358-3], evarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ Meghiya bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^358-1] kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: āraddhaviriyo viharissati . . . pe[^358-2] . . . anikkhittadhuro[^358-3] kusalesu dhammesu. Kalyāṇamittass'; etaṃ Meghiya bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^358-1] kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa: paññavā bhavissati . . . pe[^358-4] . . . sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā. Tena ca pana Meghiya bhikkhunā imesu pañcasu dhammesu patiṭṭhāya cattāro dhammā uttariṃ[^358-5] bhāvetabbā: asubhā bhāvetabbā rāgassa pahānāya, mettā bhāvetabbā vyāpādassa pahānāya, ānāpānasati[^358-6] bhāvetabbā vitakkūpacchedāya, aniccasaññā bhāvetabbā asmimānasamugghātāya. Aniccasaññino Meghiya anattasaññā saṇṭhāti, anattasaññī asmimānasamugghātaṃ pāpuṇāti diṭṭh'; eva dhamme nibbānan ti. IV.

1. Ekaṃ[^358-7] samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Nandako[^358-8] upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandasseti samādapeti samuttejeti sampahaṃseti.

2. Atha kho Bhagavā sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yen'; upaṭṭhānasālā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake aṭṭhāsi kathāpariyosānaṃ āgamayamāno. Atha kho Bhagavā kathāpariyosānaṃ viditvā ukkāsitvā[^358-9]


<!-- PTS Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 359]*

aggaḷaṃ ākoṭesi. Vivariṃsu kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato dvāraṃ. Atha kho Bhagavā upaṭṭhānasālaṃ pavisitvā paññatte[^359-1] āsane[^359-1] nisīdi. Nisajja kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Nandakaṃ[^359-2] etad[^359-3] avoca[^359-3] ‘dīgho kho tyāyaṃ Nandaka[^359-4] dhammapariyāyo bhikkhūnaṃ paṭibhāsi, api me piṭṭhi āgilāyati bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhitassa kathāpariyosānaṃ āgamayamānassā'; ti.

3. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Nandako[^359-5] sārajjamānarūpo[^359-6] Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘na kho[^359-7] mayaṃ[^359-8] bhante jānāma <<Bhagavā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhito>> ti, sace hi mayaṃ bhante jāneyyāma <<Bhagavā bahi dvārakoṭṭhake ṭhito>> ti, ettakaṃ[^359-9] pi[^359-10] no na ppaṭibhāseyyā'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Nandakaṃ[^359-11] sārajjamānarūpaṃ[^359-12] viditvā āyasmantaṃ Nandakaṃ[^359-11] etad avoca ‘sādhu sādhu Nandaka[^359-13], etaṃ[^359-14] kho Nandaka[^359-15] tumhākaṃ paṭirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhāya[^359-16] agārasmā anagāriyaṃ[^359-17] pabbajitānaṃ, yaṃ tumhe dhammiyā kathāya sannisīdeyyātha; sannipatitānaṃ vo nandaka dvayaṃ[^359-18] karaṇīyaṃ: dhammī[^359-19] vā[^359-20] kathā[^359-21] ariyo vā[^359-22] tuṇhībhāvo[^359-23]. Saddho ca[^359-24] Nandaka bhikkhu hoti no ca[^359-24] sīlavā; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro[^359-25] hoti, tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ <<kintāhaṃ saddho ca


<!-- PTS Page 360 -->
<a id="page-360"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 360]*

assaṃ sīlavā cā>> ti? Yato ca kho Nandaka bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti. Saddho ca Nandaka bhikkhu hoti sīlavā ca, no ca lābhī ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa[^360-1]; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro[^360-2] hoti, tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ <<kintāhaṃ saddho ca assaṃ sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassā[^360-3]>> ti? Yato ca kho Nandaka bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hoti. Saddho ca Nandaka bhikkhu hoti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa, na lābhī adhipaññādhammavipassanāya; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti. Seyyathā pi Nandaka pāṇako[^360-4] catuppādako, tassa assa[^360-5] eko pādo omako lāmako; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro assa: evam eva kho Nandaka bhikkhu[^360-6] saddho[^360-6] ca hoti sīlavā ca lābhī ca[^360-7] ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa, na lābhī adhipaññādhammavipassanāya; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena aparipūro hoti, tena taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūretabbaṃ <<kintāhaṃ saddho ca assaṃ sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa lābhī ca[^360-8] adhipaññādhammavipassanāyā>> ti? Yato ca kho Nandaka bhikkhu saddho ca hoti sīlavā ca[^360-7] lābhī[^360-9] ca[^360-9] ajjhattaṃ cetosamathassa lābhī ca[^360-7] adhipaññādhammavipassanāya; evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hotī'; ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna[^360-10] Sugato uṭṭhāyāsanā[^360-11] vihāraṃ pāvisi.

4. Atha kho āyasmā Nandako acirapakkantassa Bhagavato bhikkhū āmantesi: idān'; āvuso[^360-12] Bhagavā catūhi padehi kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakā-


<!-- PTS Page 361 -->
<a id="page-361"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 361]*

setvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘saddho ca[^361-1] Nandaka bhikkhu hoti . . .[^361-2]evaṃ so ten'; aṅgena paripūro hotī'; ti. Pañc'; ime āvuso ānisaṃsā kālena[^361-3] dhammasavane kālena[^361-3] dhammasākacchāya[^361-4]. Katame pañca?

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Yathā[^361-5] yathāvuso[^361-6] bhikkhu[^361-7] bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^361-8] . . . parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti, tathā tathāssa[^361-9] Satthā[^361-10] 'va[^361-11] piyo ca[^361-12] hoti manāpo ca garu ca[^361-13] bhāvanīyo ca[^361-14]. Ayaṃ āvuso paṭhamo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane kalena dhammasākacchāya.

6. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^361-8] . . . brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Yathā yathāvuso[^361-15] bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^361-16] . . . brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti, tathā tathā[^361-17] so[^361-17] tasmiṃ dhamme atthapaṭisaṃvedī ca hoti dhammapaṭisaṃvedī ca. Ayaṃ āvuso dutiyo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane kālena dhammasākacchāya.

7. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Yathā yathāvuso[^361-15] bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^361-18] . . . brah-


<!-- PTS Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 362]*

macariyaṃ pakāseti, tathā tathā[^362-1] so[^362-1] tasmiṃ dhamme gambhīraṃ atthapadaṃ paññāya paṭivijjha[^362-2] passati. Ayaṃ āvuso tatiyo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane kālena dhammasākacchāya.

8. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^362-3] . . . brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Yathā yathāvuso[^362-4] bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ . . . pe[^362-5] . . . brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti, tathā tathā naṃ[^362-6] sabrahmacārī[^362-7] uttariṃ[^362-8] sambhāventi ‘addhā ayam āyasmā patto[^362-9] vā pajjati[^362-10] vā'; ti. Ayaṃ āvuso catuttho ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane kālena dhammasākacchāya.

9. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyānaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjaṇaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Yathā yathāvuso[^362-4] bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe[^362-11] kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti, tattha tattha[^362-12] ye te[^362-13] bhikkhū sekhā[^362-14] appattamānasā[^362-15] anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, te[^362-16] taṃ[^362-16] dhammaṃ sutvā viriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa[^362-17] pattiyā anadhigatassa[^362-18] adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya; ye pana tattha bhikkhū arahanto khīṇāsavā vusitavanto katakaraṇīyā[^362-19] ohitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojanā sammadaññāvimuttā, te taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā diṭṭhadham-


<!-- PTS Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 363]*

masukhavihāraṃ yeva anuyuttā viharanti. Ayaṃ āvuso pañcamo ānisaṃso kālena dhammasavane kālena dhammasākacchāya. Ime kho āvuso pañca ānisaṃsā kālena dhammasavane kālena dhammasākacchāyā ti. V.

1. Cattār'; imāni bhikkhave balāni. Katamāni cattāri?

2. Paññābalaṃ viriyabalaṃ anavajjabalaṃ saṅgahabalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave paññābalaṃ?

3. Ye dhammā akusalā[^363-1] akusalasaṃkhātā[^363-2], ye dhammā kusalā[^363-1] kusalasaṃkhātā[^363-3], ye dhammā sāvajjā sāvajjasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā anavajjā anavajjasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā Kaṇhā kaṇhasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā sukkā sukkasaṃkhātā, ye[^363-4] dhammā asevitabbā asevitabbasaṃkhātā, ye[^363-4] dhammā sevitabbā sevitabbasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā nālamariyā nālamariyasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā alamariyā alamariyasaṃkhātā: tyāssa[^363-5] dhammā[^363-6] paññāya[^363-6] vodiṭṭhā[^363-7] honti[^363-8] vocaritā honti[^363-9]. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave paññābalaṃ[^363-10]. Katamañ ca bhikkhave viriyabalaṃ?

4. Ye dhammā akusalā akusalasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā sāvajjā sāvajjasaṃkhātā[^363-11], ye dhammā kaṇhā kaṇhasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā asevitabbā asevitabbasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā nālamariyā nālamariyasaṃkhātā: tesaṃ[^363-12] dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. Ye dhammā kusalā kusalasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā anavajjā anavajjasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā sukkā sukkasaṃkhātā, ye dhammā sevitabbā sevitabba-


<!-- PTS Page 364 -->
<a id="page-364"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 364]*

saṃkhātā, ye dhammā alamariyā alamariyasaṃkhātā: tesaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave viriyabalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave anavajjabalaṃ?

5. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako anavajjena kāyakammena samannāgato hoti, anavajjena vacīkammena samannāgato hoti, anavajjena manokammena samannāgato hoti. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave anavajjabalaṃ. Katamañ ca bhikkhave saṅgahabalaṃ?

6. Cattār'; imāni[^364-1] bhikkhave[^364-1] saṅgahavatthūni: dānaṃ peyyavajjaṃ[^364-2] atthacariyā[^364-3] samānattatā[^364-4]. Etad[^364-5] aggaṃ[^364-5] bhikkhave dānānaṃ, yad idaṃ dhammadānaṃ. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave peyyavajjānaṃ[^364-2], yad idaṃ atthikassa ohitasotassa punappunaṃ dhammaṃ deseti. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave atthacariyānaṃ, yad idaṃ assaddhaṃ saddhāsampadāya samādapeti niveseti patiṭṭhāpeti[^364-6], dussīlaṃ[^364-7] sīlasampadāya samādapeti[^364-8] niveseti patiṭṭhāpeti[^364-6], maccariṃ[^364-9] cāgasampadāya samādapeti[^364-8] niveset patiṭṭhāpeti[^364-6], duppaññaṃ paññāsampadāya samādapeti niveseti patiṭṭhāpeti[^364-6]. Etad aggaṃ bhikkhave samānattatānaṃ, yad idaṃ sotāpanno[^364-10] sotāpannassa samānatto sakadāgāmī[^364-11] sakadāgāmissa samānatto anāgāmī[^364-11] anāgāmissa samānatto arahaṃ[^364-12] arahato samānatto. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave saṅgahabalaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave cattāri balānī ti[^364-13].

7. Imehi kho bhikkhave catūhi balehi samannāgato ariyasāvako pañca bhayāni samatikkanto hoti[^364-14]. Katamāni pañca?

8. Ājīvikabhayaṃ[^364-15] asilokabhayaṃ parisasārajjabhayaṃ[^364-16]


<!-- PTS Page 365 -->
<a id="page-365"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 365]*

maraṇabhayaṃ duggatibhayaṃ. Sa[^365-1] kho so[^365-2] bhikkhave ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati:--

9. Nāhaṃ[^365-3] ājīvikabhayassa bhāyāmi. Kissāhaṃ ājīvikabhayassa bhāyissāmi? Atthi me cattāri balāni: paññābalaṃ viriyabalaṃ anavajjabalaṃ saṅgahabalaṃ. Duppañño kho ājīvikabhayassa bhāyeyya, kusīto[^365-4] ājīvikabhayassa bhāyeyya, sāvajjakāyakammanta[^365-5]-vacīkammanta[^365-5] - manokammanto[^365-6] ājīvikabhayassa bhāyeyya, asaṅgahako ājīvikabhayassa bhāyeyya. Nāhaṃ asilokabhayassa bhāyāmi . . . pe[^365-7] . . . Nāhaṃ parisasārajjabhayassa[^365-8] bhāyāmi . . . Nāhaṃ maraṇabhayassa bhāyāmi . . . Nāhaṃ duggatibhayassa bhāyāmi. Kissāhaṃ duggatibhayassa bhāyissāmi? Atthi me cattāri balāni: paññābalaṃ viriyabalaṃ anavajjabalaṃ saṅgahabalaṃ. Duppañño kho duggatibhayassa bhāyeyya, kusīto duggatibhayassa bhāyeyya, sāvajjakāyakammanta[^365-5]-vacīkammanta[^365-5]-manokammanto[^365-6] duggatibhayassa bhāyeyya, asaṅgahako duggatibhayassa bhāyeyya. Imehi kho bhikkhave catūhi balehi samannāgato ariyasāvako imāni pañca bhayāni samatikkanto hotī ti. VI.

1. Tatra kho āyasmā Sāriputto bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso[^365-9] bhikkhavo ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Sāriputtassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Sāriputto etad avoca:--

2. Puggalo pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pi; cīvaram pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbaṃ: sevitabbam pi asevitabbam pi; piṇḍapāto pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pi; senāsanam pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbaṃ: sevitabbam pi asevitabbam pi; gāmanigamo pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo


<!-- PTS Page 366 -->
<a id="page-366"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 366]*

pi asevitabbo pi; janapadapadeso pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pi[^366-1].

3. Puggalo pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tattha yaṃ[^366-2] jaññā puggalaṃ ‘imaṃ kho me puggalaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; ye ca[^366-3] kho[^366-4] me pabbajitena jīvitaparikkhārā[^366-5] samudānetabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārā, te ca kasirena[^366-6] samudāgacchanti[^366-7]; yassa c'; amhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ[^366-8] pabbajito, so ca[^366-9] me sāmaññattho na bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī[^366-10] 'ti, tenāvuso puggalena so puggalo[^366-11] rattibhāgaṃ vā divasabhāgaṃ vā[^366-12] anāpucchā[^366-13] pakkamitabbaṃ[^366-14] nānubandhitabbo[^366-15] Tattha yaṃ[^366-2] jaññā puggalaṃ ‘imaṃ kho me puggalaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti[^366-16], kusalā dhammā parihāyanti[^366-17]; ye[^366-18] ca[^366-18] kho me pabbajitena jīvitaparikkhārā[^366-5] samudānetabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārā, te[^366-19] ca[^366-19] appakasirena samudāgacchanti; yassa c'; amhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito, so ca me sāmaññattho na[^366-20] bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī[^366-10]'; ti, tenāvuso puggalena so puggalo saṃkhā pi āpucchā[^366-21] pakkamitabbaṃ[^366-22] nānubandhitabbo[^366-20]. Tattha yaṃ jaññā puggalaṃ ‘imaṃ kho me puggalaṃ sevato aku-


<!-- PTS Page 367 -->
<a id="page-367"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 367]*

salā dhammā parihāyanti[^367-1], kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti; ye ca kho me pabbajitena jīvitaparikkhārā samudānetabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārā, te ca[^367-2] appakasirena[^367-2] samudāgacchanti; yassa c'; amhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito, so ca me sāmaññattho bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī[^367-3] 'ti, tenāvuso puggalena so puggalo saṃkhā[^367-4] pi[^367-5] anubandhitabbo na[^367-6] pakkamitabbaṃ[^367-7]. Tattha yaṃ[^367-8] jaññā puggalaṃ ‘imaṃ kho me[^367-9] puggalaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti; ye ca[^367-10] kho[^367-11] me pabbajitena jīvitaparikkhārā samudānetabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārā, te ca[^367-12] appakasirena samudāgacchanti; yassa c'; amhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajito, so ca me sāmaññattho bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī'; ti, tenāvuso puggalena[^367-9] so[^367-9] puggalo yāvajīvaṃ anubandhitabbo na pakkamitabbaṃ[^367-13] api[^367-14] panujjamānena[^367-14]. Puggalo pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

4. Cīvaram pi[^367-15] āvuso duvidhena veditabbaṃ: sevitabbam pi asevitabbam pī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tattha yaṃ jaññā cīvaraṃ ‘idaṃ kho[^367-8] me cīvaraṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī'; ti, evarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ na sevitabbaṃ. Tattha yaṃ jaññā cīvaraṃ ‘idaṃ kho me cīvaraṃ sevato[^367-16] akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī'; ti, evarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ sevitabbaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 368 -->
<a id="page-368"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 368]*

Cīvaram pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbaṃ: sevitabbam pi asevitabbam pī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

5. Piṇḍapāto pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tattha yaṃ jaññā piṇḍapātaṃ ‘imaṃ[^368-1] kho me piṇḍapātaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī'; ti, evarūpo piṇḍapāto na sevitabbo. Tattha yaṃ jaññā piṇḍapātaṃ ‘imaṃ[^368-1] kho me piṇḍapātaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī'; ti, evarūpo piṇḍapāto sevitabbo. Piṇḍapāto pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

6. Senāsanam pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbaṃ: sevitabbam pi asevitabbam pī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tattha yaṃ jaññā senāsanaṃ ‘idaṃ kho me senāsanaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī 'ti, evarūpaṃ senāsanaṃ na sevitabbaṃ. Tattha yaṃ jaññā senāsanaṃ ‘idaṃ kho me senāsanaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī' ti, evarūpaṃ senāsanaṃ sevitabbaṃ. Senāsanam pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbaṃ: sevitabbam pi asevitabbam pī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

7. Gāmanigamo pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tattha yaṃ jaññā gāmanigamaṃ ‘imaṃ[^368-1] kho me gāmanigamaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī'; ti, evarūpo gāmanigamo[^368-2] na sevitabbo. Tattha yaṃ jaññā gāmanigamaṃ ‘imaṃ[^368-1] kho me gāma-


<!-- PTS Page 369 -->

##### *[PTS Page 369]*

nigamaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī'; ti, evarūpo gāmanigamo sevitabbo. Gāmanigamo pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

8. Janapadapadeso pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Tattha[^369-1] yaṃ jaññā janapadapadesaṃ ‘imaṃ kho me janapadapadesaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī'; ti, evarūpo janapadapadeso na sevitabbo. Tattha yaṃ jaññā janapadapadesaṃ ‘imaṃ kho me janapadapadesaṃ sevato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī'; ti, evarūpo janapadapadeso sevitabbo. Janapadapadeso pi āvuso duvidhena veditabbo: sevitabbo pi asevitabbo pī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttan ti. VII.

1. Evam[^369-2] me[^369-2] sutaṃ[^369-2]. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate. IX.

1. Nava yime bhikkhave puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ. Katame nava?

2. Arahā, arahattāya paṭipanno, anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sotāpanno, sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, puthujjano. Ime[^372-16] kho bhikkhave nava puggalā santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmin ti.

##### *[PTS Page 373]*

X.

1. Nava yime bhikkhave puggalā āhuneyyā pāhuneyyā dakkhiṇeyyā añjalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa. Katame nava?

2. Arahā[^373-1], arahattāya paṭipanno, anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno, sotāpanno, sotāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipanno[^373-2], gotrabhū. Ime kho bhikkhave nava puggalā āhuneyyā . . . pe . . . anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. Sambodhavaggo[^373-3] paṭhamo. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^373-4]: Sambodhi nissayo c'; eva Meghiyaṃ[^373-5] Nandakaṃ[^373-6] balaṃ Sevanā Sutavā Sajjho puggalo āhuneyyo cā ti. XI[^373-7].

1. Evam[^373-8] me[^373-8] sutaṃ[^373-8]. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘vuttho[^373-9] me bhante Sāvatthiyaṃ vassāvāso, icchām'; ahaṃ bhante janapadacārikaṃ pakkamitun' ti. ‘Yassa dāni tvaṃ Sāriputta kālaṃ maññasī'; ti. Atha kho[^373-10] āyasmā Sāriputto uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi.


<!-- PTS Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 374]*

2. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu acirapakkante āyasmante Sāriputte Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘āyasmā maṃ bhante Sāriputto āsajja[^374-1] appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkanto'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantesi ‘ehi tvaṃ bhikkhu, mama vacanena Sāriputtaṃ āmantehi[^374-2]: Satthā taṃ āvuso Sāriputta āmantetī[^374-3]'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho so bhikkhu Bhagavato paṭissutvā[^374-4] yenāyasmā Sāriputto ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ etad avoca ‘Satthā taṃ āvuso Sāriputta āmantetī'; ti. ‘Evam āvuso'; ti kho āyasmā Sāriputto tassa bhikkhuno paccassosi. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā ca Mahāmoggallāno āyasmā ca Ānando avāpuraṇaṃ[^374-5] ādāya vihārena[^374-6] vihāraṃ[^374-6] anvāhiṇḍanti[^374-7] ‘abhikkhamathāyasmanto[^374-8] abhikkhamathāyasmanto, idān'; āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavato sammukhā sīhanādaṃ nadissatī'; ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca ‘idha te Sāriputta aññataro sabrahmacārī khīyadhammaṃ āpanno: āyasmā maṃ bhante Sāriputto āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkanto'; ti.

4. Yassa nūna bhante kāye kāyagatā sati anupaṭṭhitā assa, so idha aññataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkameyya. Seyyathā pi bhante paṭhaviyaṃ sucim pi nikkhipanti asucim pi nikkhipanti gūthagatam[^374-9] pi nikkhipanti muttagatam[^374-10] pi nikkhipanti kheḷagatam pi nikkhipanti[^374-11] pubbagatam pi nikkhipanti lohitagatam pi nikkhipanti, na ca tena paṭhavī aṭṭhiyati[^374-12] vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā: evam

eva kho ahaṃ bhante paṭhavīsamena cetasā viharāmi vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Seyyathā pi bhante āpasmiṃ[^375-2] sucim pi dhovanti asucim pi dhovanti gūthagatam[^375-3] pi dhovanti[^375-4] muttagatam pi dhovanti[^375-4] kheḷagatam[^375-5] pi[^375-5] dhovanti[^375-4] pubbagatam pi dhovanti[^375-4] lohitagatam pi dhovanti, na ca tena āpo aṭṭiyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā: evam eva kho ahaṃ bhante āposamena cetasā vihārāmi vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Yassa nūna bhante kāye kāyagatā sati anupaṭṭhitā assa, so idha aññataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkameyya. Seyyathā pi bhante tejo sucim pi ḍahati[^375-6] asucim pi ḍahati gūthagatam[^375-7] pi . . . muttagatam pi . . .[^375-8] kheḷagatam pi . . . pubbagatam pi . . . lohitagatam[^375-9] pi[^375-9] ḍahati, na ca tena tejo aṭṭiyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā: evam eva kho ahaṃ bhante tejosamena cetasā viharāmi vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Yassa nūna bhante kāye kāyagatā sati anupaṭṭhitā assa, so idha aññataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkameyya. Seyyathā pi bhante vāyo sucim pi upavāyati asucim pi upavāyati gūthagatam pi upavāyati[^375-10] muttagatam pi upavāyati[^375-10] kheḷagatam pi upavāyati[^375-10] pubbagatam pi upavāyati[^375-10] lohitagatam pi upavāyati, na ca tena vāyo aṭṭiyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā: evam eva kho ahaṃ bhante vāyosamena cetasā viharāmi vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Yassa nūna bhante kāye kāyagatā sati anupaṭṭhitā assa, so idha aññataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkameyya.

Yassa nūna bhante kāye kāyagatā sati anupaṭṭhitā assa, so idha aññataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkameyya. Seyyathā pi bhante usabho chinnavisāṇo sorato[^376-13] sudanto suvinīto[^376-14] rathiyāya[^376-15] rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakena siṅghāṭakaṃ anvāhiṇḍanto na kiñci hiṃsati pādena vā visāṇena vā: evam eva kho ahaṃ bhante usabhachinnavisāṇasamena cetasā viharāmi vipulena mahaggatena[^376-7] appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena. Yassa nūna bhante kāye kāyagatā sati anupaṭṭhitā assa, so idha aññataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsajja appaṭinissajja cārikaṃ pakkameyya. Seyyathā pi bhante itthi vā puriso vā daharo vā[^376-16] yuvā vā[^376-3] maṇḍanakajātiko[^376-17] sīsaṃ[^376-3] nahāto[^376-18] ahikuṇapena vā

5. Atha kho[^377-1] so bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā Bhagavato pādesu[^377-4] sirasā nipatitvā Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘accayo maṃ bhante accagamā[^377-5] yathābālaṃ[^377-6] yathāmūḷhaṃ yathā-akusalaṃ, yo 'haṃ[^377-7] āyasamantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ asatā tucchā musā abhūtena abbhācikkhiṃ[^377-8]; tassa me bhante Bhagavā accayaṃ accayato paṭiggaṇhātu[^377-9] āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā'; ti. ‘Taggha tvaṃ[^377-10] bhikkhu accayo accagamā[^377-11] yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yo tvaṃ Sāriputtaṃ[^377-12] asatā tucchā musā abhūtena abbhācikkhi; yato ca kho tvaṃ bhikkhu accayaṃ accayato[^377-13] disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarosi, tan te[^377-14] mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, vuḍḍhi h'; esā[^377-15] bhikkhu ariyassa vinaye yo accayaṃ accayato disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikaroti āyatiṃ[^377-16] saṃvaraṃ āpajjatī'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 378 -->
