##### *[PTS Page 378]*

6. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ āmantesi ‘khama Sāriputta imassa moghapurisassa, purāssa[^378-1] tatth' eva[^378-2] sattadhā muddhā phalissatī[^378-3]'; ti. ‘Khamām'; ahaṃ bhante tassa āyasmato, sace maṃ[^378-4] so āyasmā evam āha: khamatu ca[^378-5] me so āyasmā'; ti. XII.

1. Ekaṃ[^378-6] samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiṃ[^378-7] piṇḍāya pāvisi. Atha kho āyasmato Sāriputtassa etad ahosi ‘atippago kho tāva Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ, yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi.

2. Tena kho pana samayena tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi ‘yo hi koci āvuso sa-upādiseso kālaṃ karoti, sabbo so aparimutto nirayā aparimutto tiracchānayoniyā aparimutto pittivisayā[^378-8] aparimutto apāyaduggativinipātā'; ti.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandi na ppaṭikkosi, anabhinanditvā appaṭikkositvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi ‘Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī[^378-9]'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā


<!-- PTS Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 379]*

pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca: Idhāhaṃ bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Sāvatthiṃ[^379-1] piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ[^379-2]; tassa mayhaṃ bhante etad ahosi ‘atippago kho tāva Sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ, yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan[^379-3]'; ti; atha khvāhaṃ[^379-4] bhante yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkamiṃ[^379-5], upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodiṃ[^379-6], sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃ[^379-7]; tena kho pana bhante[^379-8] samayena tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ sannisinnānaṃ sannipatitānaṃ ayam antarākathā udapādi ‘yo hi koci āvuso sa-upādiseso kālaṃ karoti, sabbo so aparimutto[^379-9] nirayā[^379-8] aparimutto tiracchānayoniyā aparimutto pittivisayā[^379-10] aparimutto apāyaduggativinipātā[^379-11]'; ti; atha khvāhaṃ[^379-4] bhante tesaṃ aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ neva abhinandiṃ na ppaṭikkosiṃ; anabhinanditvā appaṭikkositvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmiṃ[^379-12] ‘Bhagavato santike etassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī[^379-13]'; ti.

4. Keci[^379-14] Sāriputta aññatitthiyā paribbājakā bālā avyattā, keci[^379-15] sa-upādisesaṃ[^379-16] vā sa-upādiseso[^379-17] ti jānissanti, anupādisesaṃ vā anupādiseso[^379-17] ti jānissanti. Nava yime[^379-18] Sāriputta puggalā sa-upādisesā kālaṃ kurumānā parimuttā nirayā parimuttā tiracchānayoniyā parimuttā pittivisayā[^379-19] parimuttā apāyaduggativinipātā. Katame nava?


<!-- PTS Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 380]*

5. Idha Sāriputta ekacco puggalo sīlesu paripūrakārī[^380-1] hoti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī[^380-2], paññāyā na[^380-3] paripūrakārī[^380-3]. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hoti. Ayaṃ Sāriputta paṭhamo puggalo sa-upādiseso kālaṃ kurumāno parimutto nirayā parimutto tiracchānayoniyā parimutto pittivisayā[^380-4] parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā.

6. Puna ca paraṃ Sāriputta idh'; ekacco puggalo sīlesu paripūrakārī hoti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī, paññāya na paripūrakārī. So pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā upahaccaparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^380-5] asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^380-6] sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hoti . . .[^380-6] uddhaṃsoto hoti akaniṭṭhagāmī. Ayaṃ Sāriputta pañcamo puggalo sa-upādiseso kālaṃ kurumāno parimutto nirayā parimutto tiracchānayoniyā parimutto pittivisayā[^380-4] parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā[^380-7].

7. Puna ca paraṃ Sāriputta idh'; ekacco puggalo sīlesu paripūrakārī hoti, samādhismiṃ na paripūrakārī paññāya[^380-8] na[^380-8] paripūrakārī[^380-8]. So tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hoti, sakid[^380-9] eva[^380-9] imaṃ lokaṃ āgantvā dukkhass'; antaṃ karoti. Ayaṃ Sāriputta chaṭṭho puggalo sa-upādiseso kālaṃ kurumāno parimutto nirayā . . . pe[^380-10] . . . parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā.

8. Puna ca paraṃ Sāriputta idh'; ekacco puggalo sīlesu paripūrakārī hoti, samādhismiṃ na paripūrakārī[^380-11] paññāya na paripūrakārī[^380-11]. So tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā ekabījī hoti, ekaṃ yeva mānusakaṃ[^380-12] bhavaṃ nibbattetvā


<!-- PTS Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 381]*

dukkhass'; antaṃ karoti. Ayaṃ[^381-1] Sāriputta sattamo puggalo sa-upādiseso kālaṃ kurumāno parimutto nirayā . . . pe[^381-2] . . . parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā.

9. Puna ca paraṃ Sāriputta idh'; ekacco puggalo sīlesu paripūrakārī hoti samādhismiṃ mattasokārī paññāya mattasokārī. So tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā kolaṃkolo hoti, dve vā tīṇi vā kulāni sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhass' antaṃ karoti. Ayaṃ[^381-3] Sāriputta aṭṭhamo puggalo sa-upādiseso kālaṃ kurumāno parimutto nirayā . . . pe[^381-2] . . . parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā.

10. Puna ca paraṃ Sāriputta idh'; ekacco puggalo sīlesu paripūrakārī hoti samādhismiṃ mattasokārī paññāya mattasokārī. So tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā sattakkhattuparamo[^381-4] hoti; sattakkhattuparamaṃ[^381-5] deve ca manusse[^381-6] ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhass'; antaṃ karoti. Ayaṃ Sāriputta navamo puggalo sa-upādiseso kālaṃ kurumāno parimutto nirayā parimutto tiracchānayoniyā parimutto pittivisayā[^381-7] parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā. Keci[^381-8] Sāriputta aññatitthiyā paribbājakā bālā avyattā, keci[^381-9] sa-upādisesaṃ vā sa-upādiseso[^381-10] ti jānissanti, anupādisesaṃ vā anupādiseso[^381-10] ti jānissanti. Ime kho Sāriputta nava puggalā sa-upādisesā[^381-11] kālaṃ kurumānā parimuttā nirayā parimuttā tiracchānayoniyā parimuttā pittivisayā[^381-7] parimuttā apāyaduggativinipātā. Na tāvāyaṃ[^381-12] Sāriputta dhammapariyāyo paṭibhāsi bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mā yimaṃ[^381-13] dhammapariyāyaṃ sutvā pamādaṃ āhariṃsu[^381-14],


<!-- PTS Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 382]*

api ca mayā Sāriputta[^382-1] dhammapariyāyo[^382-2] pañhādhippāyena bhāsito ti. XIII.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Mahākoṭṭhito[^382-3] yenāyasmā Sāriputto ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā[^382-4] Sāriputtena saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Mahākoṭṭhito āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ etad avoca: kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ[^382-5] diṭṭhadhammavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ samparāyavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kiṃ panāvuso[^382-6] Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ samparāyavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ sukhavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ dukkhavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kiṃ panāvuso[^382-7] Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ dukkhavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ sukhavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ[^382-8] kammaṃ paripakkavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ aparipakkavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kiṃ panāvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ aparipakkavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ paripakkavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ bahuvedaniyaṃ[^382-9], taṃ me kammaṃ appavedaniyaṃ[^382-9] hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kiṃ panāvuso[^382-6]


<!-- PTS Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 383]*

Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ appavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ bahuvedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa[^383-1] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ vedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ avedaniyaṃ[^383-2] hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso. -- Kiṃ[^383-3] panāvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ avedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ vedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? No h'; idaṃ āvuso.

2. Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ samparāyavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kiṃ[^383-4] panāvuso[^383-4] Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ samparāyavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ sukhavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ dukkhavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa[^383-5] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso' ti vadesi. Kiṃ panāvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ dukkhavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ sukhavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa[^383-6] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ paripakkavedaniyaṃ[^383-7], taṃ me kammaṃ aparipakkavedaniyaṃ[^383-7] hotū'; ti etassa[^383-8] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kiṃ panāvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ aparipakkavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ paripakkavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa[^383-8] atthāya Bhagavati


<!-- PTS Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 384]*

brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ bahuvedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ appavedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa[^384-1] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kiṃ panāvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ appavedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ bahuvedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa[^384-1] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h' idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kin nu kho āvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ vedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ avedaniyaṃ hotū' ti etassa[^384-2] atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Kiṃ[^384-3] panāvuso Sāriputta ‘yaṃ kammaṃ avedaniyaṃ, taṃ me kammaṃ vedaniyaṃ hotū'; ti etassa atthāya Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti: iti puṭṭho samāno ‘no h'; idaṃ āvuso'; ti vadesi. Atha kim atthaṃ carah'; āvuso[^384-4] Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti?

3. Yaṃ khvassa[^384-5] āvuso aññātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisametaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya[^384-6] abhisamayāya[^384-7] Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti[^384-8]. Kim[^384-9] pan'; assa āvuso aññātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisametaṃ, yassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya[^384-10] Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti? ‘Idaṃ dukkhan'; ti khvassa[^384-11] āvuso aññātaṃ[^384-12] adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisametaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya[^384-7] Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti[^384-13].


<!-- PTS Page 385 -->
<a id="page-385"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 385]*

‘Ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo'; ti khvassa[^385-1] āvuso . . . pe[^385-2] . . . ‘Ayaṃ dukkhanirodho'; ti khvassa[^385-1] āvuso . . .[^385-3] ‘Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā'; ti khvassa[^385-1] āvuso aññātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisametaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya[^385-4] Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti[^385-5]. Idaṃ[^385-6] khvassa[^385-1] āvuso aññātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisametaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya[^385-4] Bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti. XIV.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Samiddhi yenāyasamā Dāriputto ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantaṃ Samiddhiṃ āyasmā Sāriputto etad avoca ‘kimārammaṇā Samiddhi purisassa saṅkappavitakkā uppajjantī'; ti? ‘Nāmarūpārammaṇā bhante'; ti. ‘Te[^385-7] pana[^385-7] Samiddhi kva[^385-8] nānattaṃ gacchantī'; ti? ‘Dhātūsu[^385-9] bhante ti. ‘Te[^385-7] pana[^385-7] Samiddhi kiṃsamudayā'; ti? ‘Phassasamudayā bhante'; ti. ‘Te pana Samiddhi kiṃsamosaraṇā' ti? ‘Vedanāsamosaraṇā bhante'; ti. ‘Te pana Samiddhi kiṃpamukhā'; ti? ‘Samādhipamukhā bhante'; ti. ‘Te pana Samiddhi kiṃ-adhipateyyā[^385-10]'; ti? ‘Satādhipateyyā bhante' ti. ‘Te pana Samiddhi kiṃ-uttarā'; ti? ‘Paññuttarā bhante' ti. ‘Te pana Samiddhi kiṃsārā'; ti? ‘Vimuttisārā bhante' ti. ‘Te[^385-11] pana[^385-11] Samiddhi kiṃ-ogadhā'; ti? ‘Amatogadhā bhante'; ti.

2. ‘Kimārammaṇā Samiddhi purisassa saṅkappavitakkā uppajjantī'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘nāmarūpārammaṇā bhante' ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kva[^385-12] nānattaṃ gacchantī'; ti


<!-- PTS Page 386 -->
<a id="page-386"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 386]*

iti puṭṭho samāno ‘dhātūsu bhante'; ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃsamudayā'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘phassasamudayā bhante'; ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃsamosaraṇā'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘vedanāsamosaraṇā bhante'; ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃpamukhā'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘samādhipamukhā bhante'; ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃ-adhipateyyā[^386-1]'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘satādhipateyyā bhante'; ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃ-uttarā'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘paññuttarā bhante'; ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃsārā'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘vimuttisārā bhante' ti vadesi, ‘te pana Samiddhi kiṃ-ogadhā'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘amatogadhā bhante'; ti vadesi. Sādhu sādhu Samiddhi, sādhu kho tvaṃ Samiddhi pañhaṃ[^386-2] puṭṭho vissajjesi[^386-3], tena ca[^386-4] mā maññī[^386-5] ti. XV.

1. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave gaṇḍo anekavassagaṇiko, tass' assu[^386-6] nava vaṇamukhāni nava abhedanamukhāni, tato yaṃ kiñci pagghareyya, asuci[^386-7] yeva[^386-7] pagghareyya, duggandhaṃ yeva pagghareyya, jegucchiyaṃ[^386-8] yeva pagghareyya, yaṃ kiñci pasaveyya, asuci yeva pasaveyya, duggandhaṃ yeva pasaveyya, jegucchiyaṃ yeva pasaveyya.

2. Gaṇḍo[^386-9] ti kho bhikkhave imass'; etaṃ[^386-10] cātummahābhūtikassa[^386-11] kāyassa adhivacanaṃ mātāpettikasambhavassa[^386-12] odanakummāsupacayassa[^386-13] aniccucchādanaparimaddanabhedanaviddhaṃsanadhammassa[^386-14], tassa[^386-15] nava vaṇamukhāni nava abhedanamukhāni, tato yaṃ kiñci paggharati, asuci yeva paggharati, duggandhaṃ yeva paggharati, jegucchiyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 387 -->
<a id="page-387"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 387]*

yeva paggharati, yaṃkiñci pasavati[^387-1], asuci yeva pasavati, duggandhaṃ yeva pasavati, jegucchiyaṃ yeva pasavati. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave imasmiṃ kāye nibbindathā ti. XVI.

1. Nava yimā bhikkhave saññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā. Katamā nava?

2. Asubhasaññā, maraṇasaññā, āhāre paṭikkūlasaññā, sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā[^387-2], aniccasaññā, anicce dukkhasaññā, dukkhe anattasaññā, pahānasaññā, virāgasaññā. Imā kho bhikkhave nava saññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā amatogadhā amatapariyosānā ti. XVII.

1. Navahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā nālaṃ nisīdituṃ. Katamehi navahi?

2. Na manāpena paccuṭṭhenti, na manāpena abhivādenti, na manāpena āsanaṃ denti, santam assa parigūhanti[^387-3], bahukam pi thokaṃ denti, paṇītam pi lūkhaṃ denti, asakkaccaṃ denti no sakkaccaṃ, na[^387-4] upanisīdanti dhammasavanāya, bhāsitassa[^387-5] na rasīyanti[^387-6]. Imehi kho bhikkhave navah'; aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā nālaṃ nisīdituṃ[^387-7].

3. Navahi bhikkhave aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā alaṃ nisīdituṃ. Katamehi navahi?

4. Manāpena paccuṭṭhenti, manāpena abhivādenti, manāpena āsanaṃ denti, santam assa na parigūhanti[^387-3], bahukam


<!-- PTS Page 388 -->
<a id="page-388"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 388]*

pi bahukaṃ[^388-1] denti, paṇītam pi paṇītaṃ denti, sakkaccaṃ denti no asakkaccaṃ, upanisīdanti dhammasavanāya, bhāsitassa[^388-2] rasīyanti[^388-3]. Imehi kho bhikkhave navah'; aṅgehi samannāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā alaṃ nisīditun ti. XVIII.

1. Navah'[^388-4] aṅgehi samannāgato bhikkhave uposatho upavuttho[^388-5] mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro. Kathaṃ upavuttho ca[^388-6] bhikkhave navah'; aṅgehi samannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro?

2. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā lajjī dayāpannā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī[^388-7] viharanti, aham p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi, iminā p'; aṅgena[^388-8] arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā paṭhamena aṅgena[^388-9] samannāgato hoti.

3. . . . [^388-10] ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭiviratā dinnādāyī dinnapāṭikhaṅkhī athenena sucibhūtena attanā viharanti, aham p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato dinnādāyī dinnapāṭikhaṅkhī athenena sucibhūtena attanā viharāmi, iminā p'; aṅgena arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā dutiyena aṅgena samannāgato hoti.

Iminā tatiyena aṅgena samannāgato hoti.

5. . . . ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratā saccavādī saccasandhā thetā paccayikā avisaṃvādakā lokassa, aham p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato saccavādī saccasandho theto paccayiko avisaṃvādako lokassa, iminā p'; aṅgena arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. Iminā catutthena aṅgena samannāgato hoti.

6. . . . ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā, aham p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmeravamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato, iminā p'; aṅgena arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī'; ti. 8. . . . ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahanto naccagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilepanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā, aham p'; ajja imañ ca rattiṃ imañ ca divasaṃ naccagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilepanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭivirato, iminā p'; aṅgena arahataṃ anukaromi, uposatho ca me upavuttho bhavissatī' ti. 10. Idha[^390-3] bhikkhave[^390-3] ariyasāvako[^390-3] mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ . . . tathā tatiyaṃ . . . tathā catutthaṃ[^390-4] . . . iti uddham adho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbatthatāya[^390-5] sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ mettāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena pharitvā viharati, iminā[^390-6] navamena aṅgena[^390-2] samannāgato hoti[^390-7]. Evañ ca[^390-8] upavuttho kho bhikkhave navaṅgasamannāgato uposatho mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso mahājutiko mahāvipphāro ti. XIX.

1. Imañ ca[^390-8] bhikkhave rattiṃ sambahulā devatā[^390-9] abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṃ Jetavanaṃ obhāsetvā yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho bhikkhave tā devatā[^390-10] maṃ etad avocuṃ ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu no bhante pubbe manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā agārāni, tā[^390-11] mayaṃ bhante paccuṭṭhimha[^390-12], no ca kho abhivādimha, tā[^390-11] mayaṃ bhante aparipuṇṇakammantā vippaṭisāriniyo pacchānutāpiniyo[^390-13] hīnaṃ kāyaṃ upapannā[^390-14]'; ti.

2. Aparā pi maṃ bhikkhave sambahulā devatā upasaṅkamitvā etad avocuṃ ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu no bhante pubbe manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā[^391-1] agārāni, tā mayaṃ bhante paccuṭṭhimha[^391-2] abhivādimha[^391-2], no ca kho[^391-3] āsanaṃ adamha[^391-4], tā[^391-5] mayaṃ bhante aparipuṇṇakammantā vippaṭisāriniyo pacchānutāpiniyo hīnaṃ kāyaṃ upapannā'; ti.

3. Aparā pi maṃ bhikkhave sambahulā devatā upasaṅkamitvā etad avocuṃ ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu no bhante pubbe manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā agārāni, tā[^391-6] mayaṃ bhante paccuṭṭhimha ca abhivādimha[^391-7] ca āsanañ ca[^391-8] adamha[^391-9], no ca kho yathāsattiṃ[^391-10] yathābalaṃ[^391-11] saṃvibhajimha . . . pe[^391-12] . . . yathāsattiṃ[^391-8] yathābalaṃ[^391-8] saṃvibhajimha[^391-8], no ca kho upanisīdimha dhammasavanāya . . .[^391-13] upanisīdimha[^391-14] dhammasavanāya, no ca kho ohitasotā dhammaṃ suṇimha[^391-15] . . .[^391-13] ohitasotā dhammaṃ suṇimha, no ca kho sutvā dhammaṃ dārayimha . . .[^391-13] sutvā[^391-16] ca[^391-16] dhammaṃ[^391-16] dhārayimha[^391-16], no ca kho dhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhimha . . .[^391-13] dhatānañ[^391-17] ca[^391-17] dhammānaṃ[^391-17] atthaṃ[^391-17] upaparikkhimha[^391-17], no ca kho attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipajjimha, tā[^391-18] mayaṃ bhante aparipuṇṇakammantā vippaṭisāriniyo pacchānutāpiniyo[^391-19] hīnaṃ kāyaṃ upapannā'; ti.

4. Aparā pi maṃ bhikkhave sambahulā devatā upasaṅkamitvā etad avocuṃ ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu no bhante pubbe manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā[^391-20] agārāni, tā[^391-6] mayaṃ bhante paccuṭṭhimha[^391-21] abhivādimha[^391-22] āsanaṃ[^391-22] adamha[^391-23], yathā-


<!-- PTS Page 392 -->

##### *[PTS Page 392]*

sattiṃ[^392-1] yathābalaṃ saṃvibhajimha upanisīdimha[^392-2] dhammasavanāya, ohitasotā ca dhammaṃ suṇimha, sutvā[^392-3] ca[^392-4] dhammaṃ dhārayimha, dhatānañ[^392-5] ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhimha, attham aññāya dhammam aññāya dhammānudhammapaṭipajjimha, tā[^392-6] mayaṃ bhante paripuṇṇakammantā avippaṭisāriniyo apacchānutāpiniyo paṇītaṃ kāyaṃ upapannā'; ti. Etāni bhikkhave rukkhamūlāni etāni suññāgārāni, jhāyatha bhikkhave mā pamādatha[^392-7], mā pacchā vippaṭisārino ahuvattha, seyyathā pi tā purimikā devatā ti. XX.

1. Ekaṃ[^392-8] samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca: api nu te gahapati kule dānaṃ diyyatī ti? Diyyati me bhante kule[^392-9] dānaṃ[^392-9], tañ ca kho[^392-9] lūkhaṃ kaṇājakaṃ[^392-10] bilaṅgadutiyan ti.

2. Lūkhañ ce[^392-11] pi gahapati dānaṃ deti paṇītaṃ vā, tañ ca asakkaccaṃ deti, acittikatvā[^392-12] deti, asahatthā deti, apaviddhaṃ[^392-13] deti, anāgamanadiṭṭhiko deti; yattha yattha tassa tassa[^392-14] dānassa vipāko nibbattati, na uḷārāya bhattabhogāya cittaṃ namati, na uḷārāya vatthabhogāya cittaṃ namati, na uḷārāya yānabhogāya cittaṃ namati, na uḷāresu pañcasu kāmaguṇesu bhogāya cittaṃ namati; ye pi 'ssa te

3. Lūkhañ ce[^393-5] pi gahapati dānaṃ deti paṇītaṃ vā, tañ ca sakkaccaṃ deti, cittikatvā[^393-6] deti. sahatthā deti, anapaviddhaṃ[^393-7] deti, āgamanadiṭṭhiko deti; yattha yattha tassa tassa[^393-8] dānassa vipāko nibbattati, uḷārāya bhattabhogāya cittaṃ namati, ulārāya vatthabhogāya cittaṃ namati, uḷārāya yānabhogāya cittaṃ namati, uḷāresu pañcasu kāmaguṇesu bhogāya cittaṃ namati; ye pi 'ssa te honti puttā ti vā dārā ti vā dāsā ti vā pessā ti vā kammakarā ti vā, te pi sussusanti sotaṃ odahanti aññācittaṃ upaṭṭhapenti[^393-9]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Evaṃ h'; etaṃ[^393-3] gahapati hoti sakkaccakatānaṃ[^393-10] kammānaṃ vipāko.

4. Bhūtapubbaṃ gahapati Velāmo nāma brāhmaṇo ahosi. So evarūpaṃ dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ: caturāsīti suvaṇṇapātisahassāni adāsi rūpiyapūrāni, caturāsīti rūpiyapātisahassāni adāsi suvaṇṇapūrāni, caturāsīti kaṃsapātisahassāni adāsi hiraññapūrāni, caturāsīti hatthisahassāni adāsi sovaṇṇālaṃkārāni sovaṇṇadhajāni hemajālasañchannāni[^393-11], caturāsīti rathasahassāni adāsi sīhacammaparivārāni vyagghacammaparivārāni dipicammaparivārāni paṇḍukambalaparivārāni[^393-12] sovaṇṇālaṃkārāni sovaṇṇadhajāni hemajālasañchannāni, caturāsīti dhenusahassāni adāsi dukūlasanthanāni[^393-13] kaṃsūpadhāraṇāni[^393-14], caturāsīti kaññāsahassāni adāsi āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalāyo[^393-15], caturāsīti pallaṅkasahassāni adāsi

5. Siyā kho pana te gahapati evam assa ‘añño nūna[^394-9] tena samayena Velāmo brāhmaṇo ahosi, so taṃ dānaṃ adāsi mahādānan'; ti. Na kho pan'; etaṃ[^394-10] gahapati evaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Ahaṃ tena samayena Velāmo brāhmaṇo ahosiṃ[^394-11], ahaṃ taṃ dānaṃ adāsiṃ[^394-12] mahādānaṃ. Tasmiṃ kho pana gahapati dāne na koci dakkhiṇeyyo ahosi, na taṃ koci dakkhiṇaṃ visodheti. Yaṃ gahapati Velāmo brāhmaṇo dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ, yo c'; ekaṃ[^394-13] diṭṭhisampannaṃ bhojeyya, idaṃ tato mahapphalataraṃ. ##### *[PTS Page 396]*

veyya yo ca[^396-1] accharāsaṃghātamattam pi aniccasaññaṃ bhāveyya, idaṃ tato mahapphalataran ti. Sīhanādavaggo[^396-2] dutiyo. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^396-3]: Vuttho[^396-4] sa-upādiseso ca Koṭṭhitena[^396-5] Samiddhinā Gaṇḍasaññā kule mettā[^396-6] devatā Velāmena cā ti. XXI[^396-7].

1. Tīhi bhikkhave ṭhānehi Uttarakurukā manussā deve ca Tāvatiṃse adhigaṇhanti Jambudīpake ca manusse. Katamehi tīhi?

2. Amamā[^396-8] apariggahā niyatāyukā[^396-9] visesabhuno[^396-10] Imehi kho bhikkhave tīhi ṭhānehi Uttarakurukā manussā deve ca[^396-11] Tāvatiṃse adhigaṇhanti Jambudīpake ca[^396-11] manusse.

3. Tīhi bhikkhave ṭhānehi devā Tāvatiṃsā Uttarakuruke ca[^396-11] manusse adhigaṇhanti Jambudīpake ca manusse. Katamehi tīhi?

4. Dibbena āyunā dibbena vaṇṇena dibbena sukhena. Imehi kho bhikkhave tīhi ṭhānehi devā Tāvatiṃsā Uttarakuruke ca manusse adhigaṇhanti Jambudīpake ca manusse.

5. Tīhi bhikkhave ṭhānehi Jambudīpake manussā Uttarakuruke ca[^396-11] manusse adhigaṇhanti deve ca Tāvatiṃse. Katamehi tīhi? Surā satimanto idhabrahmacariyavāso. Imehi kho bhikkhave tīhi ṭhānehi Jambudīpakā manussā Uttarakuruke ca manusse adhigaṇhanti deve ca Tāvatiṃse ti[^396-12].


<!-- PTS Page 397 -->
<a id="page-397"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 397]*

XXII.

1. Tayo ca bhikkhave assakhaḷuṅke[^397-1] desessāmi tayo ca purisakhaḷuṅke[^397-1] tayo ca assasadasse[^397-2] tayo ca purisasadasse[^397-2] tayo ca bhadde[^397-3] assājānīye tayo ca bhadde purisājānīye. Taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ[^397-4] manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. Evam bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo[^397-5] assakhaḷuṅkā? Idha bhikkhave ekacco assakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^397-5] hoti na vaṇṇasampanno[^397-6] na ārohapariṇāhasampanno. Idha pana[^397-7] bhikkhave ekacco assakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^397-8] hoti vaṇṇasampanno[^397-9] na ārohapariṇāhasampanno. Idha pana[^397-7] bhikkhave ekacco assakhaḷuṅko[^397-10] javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca[^397-11] ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo[^397-12] assakhaḷuṅkā.

3. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo purisakhaḷuṅkā? Idha bhikkhave ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^397-8] hoti na vaṇṇasampanno na ārohapariṇāhasampanno. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^397-13] hoti vaṇṇasampanno[^397-14] na ārohapariṇāhasampanno. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca.

4. Kathañ ca bhikkhave purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^397-15] hoti na vaṇṇasampanno na ārohapariṇāhasampanno? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkhan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodho'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti.


<!-- PTS Page 398 -->
<a id="page-398"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 398]*

Idam assa javasmiṃ vadāmi. Abhidhamme kho pana abhivinaye pañhaṃ puṭṭho saṃsādeti[^398-1], no vissajjeti[^398-2]. Idam assa na vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi. Na[^398-3] kho pana lābhī hoti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Idam assa na ārohapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno hoti na vaṇṇasampanno na ārohapariṇāhasampanno.

5. Kathañ ca bhikkhave purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^398-4] hoti vaṇṇasampanno[^398-5] na ārohapariṇāhasampanno? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkhan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti . . . pe[^398-6] . . . ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Idam assa javasmiṃ vadāmi. Abhidhamme kho pana abhivinaye pañhaṃ puṭṭho vissajjeti[^398-7], no saṃsādeti[^398-8]. Idam assa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi. Na kho pana lābhī hoti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Idam assa na ārohapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno[^398-4] hoti vaṇṇasampanno[^398-5] na ārohapariṇāhasampanno.

6. Kathañ ca bhikkhave purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno ca[^398-9] hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkhan'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti . . . pe[^398-10] . . . ‘ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā'; ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Idam assa javasmiṃ vadāmi. Abhidhamme kho pana abhivinaye pañhaṃ puṭṭho vissajjeti[^398-11], no saṃsādeti[^398-12]. Idam assa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi.


<!-- PTS Page 399 -->
<a id="page-399"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 399]*

Lābhī kho pana hoti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Idam assa ārohapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave purisakhaḷuṅko javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo purisakhaḷuṅkā.

7. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo assasadassā[^399-1]? Idha bhikkhave ekacco assasadasso . . . pe[^399-2] . . . javasampanno ca[^399-3] hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca[^399-4]. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo assa sadassā.

8. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo purisasadassā? Idha bhikkhave ekacco purisasadasso . . . pe[^399-5] . . . javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca.

9. Kathañ ca bhikkhave purisasadasso[^399-6] javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti, tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā. Idam assa javasmiṃ vadāmi. Abhidhamme kho pana abhivinaye pañhaṃ puṭṭho vissajjeti[^399-7], no saṃsādeti[^399-8]. Idam assa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi. Lābhī kho pana hoti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Idam assa ārohapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave purisasadasso javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo purisasadassā.

10. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo bhaddā[^399-9] assājānīyā? Idha bhikkhave ekacco bhaddo assājānīyo . . . pe[^399-10] . . . javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca.


<!-- PTS Page 400 -->
<a id="page-400"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 400]*

Ime kho bhikkhave tayo bhaddā assājānīyā.

11. Katame ca bhikkhave tayo bhaddā purisājānīyā? Idha bhikkhave ekacco bhaddo purisājānīyo . . . pe[^400-1] . . . javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca.

12. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhaddo purisājānīyo . . . pe[^400-1] . . . javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu[^400-2] āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Idam assa javasmiṃ vadāmi. Abhidhamme kho pana abhivinaye pañhaṃ puṭṭho vissajjeti[^400-3], no saṃsādeti[^400-4]. Idam assa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi. Lābhī kho pana hoti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Idaṃ assa ārohapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhaddo purisājānīyo javasampanno ca hoti vaṇṇasampanno ca ārohapariṇāhasampanno ca. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo bhaddā purisājānīyā ti. XXIII.

1. Nava bhikkhave taṇhāmūlake dhamme desessāmi[^400-5]. Taṃ suṇātha . . . pe[^400-6] . . . Katame ca[^400-7] bhikkhave nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā?

2. Taṇhaṃ paṭicca pariyesanā, pariyesanaṃ paṭicca lābho, lābhaṃ paṭicca vinicchayo, vinicchayaṃ paṭicca chandarāgo, chandarāgaṃ paṭicca ajjhosānaṃ[^400-8], ajjhosānaṃ paṭicca pariggaho, pariggahaṃ paṭicca macchariyaṃ[^400-9], macchariyaṃ paṭicca[^400-10] ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ[^400-11], daṇḍādāna-


<!-- PTS Page 401 -->
<a id="page-401"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 401]*

satthādānakalahaviggahavivādā[^401-1] tuvaṃtuvaṃpesuññamusāvādā[^401-2] aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti. Ime kho bhikkhave nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā ti. XXIV.

1. Nava yime bhikkhave sattāvāsā. Katame nava?

2. Santi bhikkhave sattā nānattakāyā nānattasaññino, seyyathā pi manussā ekacce ca devā[^401-3] ekacce[^401-3] ca[^401-3] vinipātikā: ayaṃ paṭhamo sattāvāso.

3. Santi bhikkhave sattā nānattakāyā ekattasaññino, seyyathā pi devā[^401-4] Brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā: ayaṃ dutiyo sattāvāso.

4. Santi bhikkhave sattā ekattakāyā nānattasaññino, seyyathā pi devā[^401-4] Ābhassarā: ayaṃ tatiyo sattāvāso.

5. Santi bhikkhave sattā ekattakāyā ekattasaññino, seyyathā pi devā Subhakiṇhā[^401-5]: ayaṃ catuttho sattāvāso.

6. Santi bhikkhave sattā asaññino appaṭisaṃvedino, seyyathā pi devā Asaññasattā[^401-6]: ayaṃ pañcamo sattāvāso.

7. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ[^401-7] atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanūpagā: ayaṃ chaṭṭho sattāvāso.

8. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanūpagā: ayaṃ sattamo sattāvāso.

9. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanūpagā: ayaṃ aṭṭhamo sattāvāso.

10. Santi bhikkhave sattā sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpagā: ayaṃ navamo sattāvāso. Ime kho bhikkhave nava sattāvāsā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 402 -->
<a id="page-402"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 402]*

XXV.

1. Yato[^402-1] kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno kallaṃ vacanāya ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti[^402-2]. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhuno paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti?

2. ‘Vītarāgaṃ me cittan'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘vītadosaṃ me cittan'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘vītamohaṃ me cittan'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘asarāgadhammaṃ me cittan'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘asadosadhammaṃ[^402-3] me cittan'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘asamohadhammaṃ me cittan'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘anāvattidhammaṃ me cittaṃ kāmabhavāyā'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘anāvattidhammaṃ me cittaṃ rūpabhavāyā'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘anāvattidhammaṃ me cittaṃ arūpabhavāyā'; ti paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti. Yato kho bhikkhave bhikkhuno paññāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno kallaṃ vacanāya ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī[^402-4]'; ti. XXVI.

1. Evam[^402-5] me[^402-5] sutaṃ[^402-5]. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca Sāriputto āyasmā ca Candikāputto Rājagahe viharanti Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe. Tatra kho āyasmā Candikāputto bhikkhū āmantesi[^402-6]: Devadatto āvuso bhikkhūnaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘yato[^402-7] kho āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ[^402-8] suparicitaṃ[^402-8] hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhuno kallaṃ veyyāka-


<!-- PTS Page 403 -->
<a id="page-403"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 403]*

raṇāya: khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī[^403-1]'; ti.

2. Evaṃ[^403-2] vutte[^403-2] āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmantaṃ Candikāputtaṃ etad avoca: na kho āvuso Candikāputta Devadatto bhikkhūnaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘yato[^403-3] kho āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhuno kallaṃ veyyākaraṇāya: khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti, evañ ca[^403-4] kho āvuso Candikāputta Devadatto bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘yato[^403-3] kho āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhuno kallaṃ veyyākaraṇāya: khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti.

3. Dutiyam pi kho . . . pe[^403-5] . . . tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Candikāputto bhikkhū āmantesi: Devadatto āvuso bhikkhūnaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘yato[^403-3] kho āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhuno veyyākaraṇāya: khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti. Tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmantaṃ Candikāputtaṃ etad avoca: na kho āvuso Candikāputta Devadatto bhikkhūnaṃ evaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘yato kho āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhuno kallaṃ veyyākaraṇāya: khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānātī'; ti, evañ[^403-6] ca kho āvuso Candikāputta Devadatto bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti ‘yato kho āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, tass'; etaṃ bhikkhuno kallaṃ veyyākaraṇāya: khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā ti pajānāmī'; ti.

4. Kathañ ca āvuso bhikkhuno cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 404 -->
<a id="page-404"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 404]*

‘Vītarāgaṃ me cittan'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘vītadosaṃ me cittan'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘vītamohaṃ me cittan'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘asarāgadhammaṃ me cittan'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘asadosadhammaṃ[^404-1] me cittan'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘asamohadhammaṃ me cittan'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘anāvattidhammaṃ me cittaṃ kāmabhavāyā'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘anāvattidhammaṃ me cittaṃ rūpabhavāyā'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti, ‘anāvattidhammaṃ me cittaṃ arūpabhavāyā'; ti cetasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hoti.

5. Evaṃ sammāvimuttacittassa[^404-2] kho[^404-3] āvuso bhikkhuno bhusā[^404-4] ce pi cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ[^404-5] āgacchanti[^404-6], nev'; assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti[^404-7], amissīkatam ev'; assa cittaṃ hoti ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ[^404-8], vayaṃ c'; assānupassati; bhusā[^404-9] ce pi sotaviññeyyā saddā . . .[^404-10] ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā . . . manoviññeyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, nev'; assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam ev' assa cittaṃ hoti ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ[^404-11], vayaṃ c'; assānupassati. Sevyathā pi āvuso silāyūpo soḷasakukkuko[^404-12], tassa assu[^404-13] aṭṭha kukkū[^404-14] heṭṭhā nemassa[^404-15] aṭṭha kukkū[^404-16] upari nemassa; atha puratthimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā[^404-17] vātavuṭṭhi, neva naṃ[^404-18] kampeyya[^404-19] na samkam-


<!-- PTS Page 405 -->
<a id="page-405"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 405]*

peyya[^405-1] na sampavedheyya; atha pacchimāya . . .[^405-2] atha uttarāya . . .[^405-3] atha dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā[^405-4] vātavuṭṭhi, neva naṃ[^405-5] kampeyya[^405-6] na samkampeyya[^405-1] na sampavedheyya. Taṃ kissa hetu? Gambhīrattā āvuso nemassa sunikhātattā silāyūpassa. Evam eva[^405-7] kho[^405-7] āvuso[^405-7] evaṃ sammāvimuttacittassa[^405-8] bhikkhuno bhusā[^405-9] ce pi cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, nev'; assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam ev'; assa cittaṃ hoti ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ[^405-10], vayaṃ c'; assānupassati; bhusā[^405-11] ce pi sotaviññeyyā saddā . . .[^405-12] ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā . . . manoviññeyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti[^405-13], nev'; assa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti, amissīkatam ev'; assa cittaṃ hoti ṭhitaṃ ānejjappattaṃ[^405-10], vayaṃ c'; assānupassatī ti. XXVII.

1. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Yato kho gahapati ariyasāvakassa pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni hoti, catūhi ca[^405-14] sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti, so ākaṅkhamāno attanā 'va attānaṃ vyākareyya: khīṇanirayo 'mhi khīṇatiracchānayoni[^405-15] khīṇapittivisayo[^405-16] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto, sotāpanno 'ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano[^405-17].


<!-- PTS Page 406 -->
<a id="page-406"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 406]*

3. Katamāni pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti? Yaṃ gahapati pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapaccayā diṭṭhadhammikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, samparāyikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, cetasikam pi dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti; pāṇātipātā paṭivirato neva diṭṭhadhammikam pi[^406-1] bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na[^406-2] samparāyikam pi[^406-1] bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na cetasikam pi[^406-1] dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti. Pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa evaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ vūpasantaṃ hoti. Yaṃ gahapati adinnādāyī . . . pe[^406-3] . . . kāmesu micchācārī . . . musāvādī . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhāyī surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānapaccayā diṭṭhadhammikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, samparāyikam pi[^406-4] bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, cetasikam pi dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti[^406-5]; surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato neva diṭṭhadhammikam pi[^406-1] bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na samparāyikam pi[^406-1] bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na cetasikam pi[^406-1] dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti. Surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa evaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ vūpasantaṃ hoti. Imāni pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti.

4. Katamehi catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti? Idha[^406-6] gahapati ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti; dhamme aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti ‘svākkhāto[^406-7] Bhagavatā dhammo sandiṭṭhiko akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^406-8] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī'; ti; saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti ‘supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, ujupaṭipanno[^406-9] Bhagavato[^406-9] sāvakasaṅgho[^406-9],


<!-- PTS Page 407 -->
<a id="page-407"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 407]*

ñāyapaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, sāmīcipaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, yad idaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, esa Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho āhuneyyo[^407-1] pāhuneyyo[^407-1] dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā'; ti; ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti[^407-2] akhaṇḍehi acchiddehi asabalehi akammāsehi bhujissehi viññuppasatthehi aparāmaṭṭhehi samādhisaṃvattanikehi. Imehi catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti.

5. Yato[^407-3] kho gahapati ariyasāvakassa imāni pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti, imehi ca[^407-4] catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti, so ākaṅkhamāno attanā 'va attānaṃ vyākareyya: khīṇanirayo 'mhi khīṇatiracchānayoni[^407-5] khīṇapittivisayo[^407-6] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto, sotāpanno 'ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti. XXVIII.

1. Yato kho bhikkhave ariyasāvakassa pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti, catūhi ca[^407-4] sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti, so ākaṅkhamāno attanā 'va attānaṃ vyākareyya: khīṇanirayo 'mhi khīṇatiracchānayoni[^407-7] khīṇapittivisayo[^407-6] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto, sotāpanno 'ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano.

4. XXIX.

1. Nava yimāni[^408-4] bhikkhave āghātavatthūni. Katamāni nava?

2. ‘Anatthaṃ me acarī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati, ‘anatthaṃ me caratī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati, ‘anatthaṃ me carissatī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati[^408-5], ‘piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ acari[^408-6] . . .[^408-7] anatthaṃ carati[^408-8] . . .[^408-7] anatthaṃ carissatī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati[^408-5], ‘appiyassa me amanāpassa atthaṃ acari[^408-6] . . .[^408-9] atthaṃ carati[^408-8] . . .[^408-9] atthaṃ carissatī'; ti āghātaṃ bandhati. Imāni kho bhikkhave nava āghātavatthūnī ti. XXX.

1. Nava yime[^408-10] bhikkhave āghātapaṭivinayā. Katame nava?

2. ‘Anatthaṃ me acari[^408-11], taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘anatthaṃ me carati[^408-12], taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘anatthaṃ me carissati[^408-13], taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ acari[^408-14] . . .[^408-7]anatthaṃ carati[^408-15] . . .[^408-7]


<!-- PTS Page 409 -->

##### *[PTS Page 409]*

anatthaṃ carissati[^409-1], taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti, ‘appiyassa me amanāpassa atthaṃ cari[^409-2] . . .[^409-3] atthaṃ carati[^409-4] . . .[^409-3]atthaṃ carissati[^409-5], taṃ kut'; ettha labbhā'; ti āghātaṃ paṭivineti. Ime kho bhikkhave nava āghātapaṭivinayā ti. XXXI.

1. Nava yime bhikkhave anupubbanirodhā. Katame nava?

2. Paṭhamaṃ[^409-6] jhānaṃ[^409-6] samāpannassa kāmasaññā[^409-7] niruddhā hoti[^409-8], dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa vitakkavicārā niruddhā honti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa pīti niruddhā hoti[^409-9], catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa assāsapassāsā niruddhā honti, ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa rūpasaññā niruddhā hoti[^409-10], viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa ākāsānañcāyatanasaññā niruddhā hoti[^409-9], ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa viññāṇañcāyatanasaññā niruddhā hoti[^409-11], nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa ākiñcaññāyatanasaññā niruddhā hoti[^409-9], saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ samāpannassa saññā ca vedanā ca niruddhā honti[^409-12]. Ime kho bhikkhave nava anupubbanirodhā ti. Sattāvāsavaggo[^409-13] tatiyo. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^409-14]: Ṭhānakhaḷuṅko[^409-15] taṇhā ca[^409-16] satta[^409-17]-saññā[^409-18] silāyūpo Dve verā dve āghātāni anupubbanirodhena cā ti.

XXXII[^410-1].

1. Nava yime bhikkhave anupubbavihārā. Katame nava?

2. Idha[^410-2] bhikkhave bhikkhu vicicc'; eva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, pītiyā[^410-3] ca virāgā . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubb'; eva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati . . . pe . . . sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto[^410-4] ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ime kho bhikkhave nava anupubbavihārā ti. XXXIII.

1. Nava yimā bhikkhave anupubbavihārasamāpattiyo desessāmi[^410-5], taṃ suṇātha . . . pe[^410-6] . . . Katamā ca bhikkhave nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyo?

2. Yattha kāmā nirujjhanti, ye ca kāme nirodhetvā nirodhetvā viharanti, addhā te āyasmanto nicchātā nibbutā

tiṇṇā pāragatā[^411-1] tadaṅgenā[^411-2] ti vadāmi. Kattha kāmā nirujjhanti, ke[^411-3] ca[^411-3] kāme nirodhetvā nirodhetvā viharanti? Aham etaṃ na jānāmi, aham etaṃ na passāmī ti iti yo evaṃ vadeyya, so evam assa vacanīyo ‘idhāvuso bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^411-4] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; ettha kāmā nirujjhanti, te ca kāme nirodhetvā nirodhetvā viharantī'; ti. Addhā bhikkhave asaṭho[^411-5] amāyāvī sādhū ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandeyya anumodeyya, sādhū ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā namassamāno pañjaliko payirupāseyya.

3. Yattha vitakkavicārā nirujjhanti, ye ca[^411-6] vitakkavicāre nirodhetvā nirodhetvā viharanti, addhā te āyasmanto nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāragatā tadaṅgenā[^411-2] ti vadāmi. Aham etaṃ na jānāmi, aham etaṃ na passāmī ti iti yo evaṃ vadeyya, so evam assa vacanīyo ‘idhāvuso bhikkhu vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^411-8] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; ettha vitakkavicārā nirujjhanti, te ca vitakkavicāre nirodhetvā nirodhetvā viharantī'; ti. Addhā bhikkhave asaṭho[^411-7] amāyāvī sādhū ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandeyya anumodeyya, sādhū ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā namassamāno pañjaliko payirupāseyya.

Aham etaṃ na jānāmi, aham[^411-7] etaṃ[^411-7] na passāmī ti iti yo evaṃ vadeyya, so evam assa vacanīyo ‘idhāvuso bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā . . . pe[^411-8] . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; ettha pīti nirujjhati, te ca pītiṃ nirodhetvā niro-

Imā[^414-3] kho bhikkhave nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyo ti XXXIV.

1. Evam[^414-4] me[^414-4] sutaṃ[^414-4]. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Sāriputto Rājagahe viharati Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe. Tatra kho āyasmā Sāriputto bhikkhū āmantesi[^414-5] ‘sukham idaṃ āvuso nibbānaṃ, sukham idaṃ āvuso nibbānan'; ti.

2. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Udāyi āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtaṃ etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 415 -->
<a id="page-415"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 415]*

Kiṃ pan'; ettha āvuso Sāriputta sukhaṃ, yad ettha natthi vedayitan ti?

3. Etad eva khv[^415-1] ettha[^415-1] āvuso[^415-1] sukhaṃ, yad ettha natthi vedayitaṃ. Pañc'; ime āvuso kāmaguṇā. Katame pañca? Cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā[^415-2] kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā[^415-3], sotaviññeyyā saddā . . . pe[^415-4] . . . ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā[^415-5] kantā manāpā piyarūpā[^415-2] kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā. Ime kho āvuso pañca kāmaguṇā. Yaṃ[^415-6] kho āvuso ime pañca kāmaguṇe paṭicca uppajjati sukhaṃ somanassaṃ, idaṃ vuccat'; āvuso kāmasukhaṃ.

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi[^415-7] . . . pe[^415-4] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato kāmasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa[^415-8] hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa te kāmasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ[^415-9] vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ[^415-10] āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ[^415-11].

5. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^415-12] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato vitakkasahagatā[^415-13] saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa[^415-14] te vitakkasahagatā


<!-- PTS Page 416 -->
<a id="page-416"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 416]*

saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.

6. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā . . . pe[^416-1] . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato pītisahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa te[^416-2] pītisahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ[^416-3] āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.

7. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā[^416-4] . . . pe[^416-5] . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato upekhāsahagatā[^416-6] saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa te upekhāsahagatā[^416-7] saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa[^416-8] hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.

8. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^416-9] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato rūpasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa


<!-- PTS Page 417 -->
<a id="page-417"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 417]*

te rūpasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.

9. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato ākāsānañcāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa te ākāsānañcāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.

10. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato viññāṇañcāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā[^417-1] pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa te viññāṇañcāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.

11. Puna[^417-2] ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Tassa ce āvuso bhikkhuno iminā vihārena viharato ākiñcaññāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi āvuso sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa te ākiñcaññāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa hoti ābādho. Yo kho panāvuso ābādho, dukkham etaṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 418 -->
<a id="page-418"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 418]*

12. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, paññāya[^418-1] c'; assa[^418-2] disvā[^418-3] āsavā parikkhīṇā honti. Iminā pi kho etaṃ āvuso pariyāyena veditabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ nibbānan ti. XXXV.

1. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave gāvī pabbateyyā bālā avyattā akhettaññū akusalā visame pabbate carituṃ, tassā[^418-4] evam assa ‘yan nūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ[^418-5] c'; eva disaṃ gaccheyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādeyyaṃ, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni piveyyan[^418-6]'; ti; sā purimaṃ pādaṃ na suppatiṭṭhitaṃ patiṭṭhāpetvā pacchimaṃ pādaṃ uddhareyya, sā na[^418-7] c'; eva agatapubbaṃ[^418-8] disaṃ gaccheyya, na ca akhāditapubbāni[^418-9] tiṇāni[^418-10] khādeyya, na ca apītapubbāni[^418-11] pānīyāni piveyya; yasmiṃ c'; assā[^418-12] pāde[^418-13] ṭhitāya[^418-14] evam assa ‘yan nūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ c'; eva disaṃ gaccheyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādeyyaṃ, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni piveyyan'; ti, tañ ca padesaṃ na sotthinā paccāgaccheyya[^418-15]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tattha hi sā bhikkhave gāvī pabbateyyā bālā avyattā akhettaññū akusalā visame pabbate carituṃ. Evam eva kho bhikkhave idh'; ekacco bhikkhu bālo avyatto akhettaññū akusalo vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . .[^418-16] paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharituṃ[^418-17]; so taṃ nimittaṃ na[^418-18] āsevati[^418-18] na bhāveti na bahulīkaroti na[^418-19] svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^418-16] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 419 -->
<a id="page-419"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 419]*

upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so na[^419-1] sakkoti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . .[^419-2] dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharituṃ; tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^419-3] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan' ti; so na sakkoti vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . .[^419-2] paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharituṃ. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu ubhato bhaṭṭho ubhato parihīno, seyyathā pi sā gāvī pabbateyyā bālā avyattā akhettaññū akusalā visame pabbate carituṃ.

2. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave gāvī pabbateyyā paṇḍitā vyattā khettaññū kusalā visame pabbate carituṃ, tassā evam assa ‘yan nūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ c'; eva disaṃ gaccheyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādeyyaṃ, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni piveyyan'; ti; sā purimaṃ pādaṃ suppatiṭṭhitaṃ patiṭṭhāpetvā pacchimaṃ pādaṃ uddhareyya, sā agatapubbañ[^419-4] c'; eva disaṃ gaccheyya, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādeyya, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni piveyya; yasmiṃ c'; assā[^419-5] pāde[^419-6] ṭhitāya evam assa ‘yan nūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ[^419-7] c'; eva disaṃ gaccheyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādeyyaṃ, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni piveyyan'; ti, tañ ca padesaṃ sotthinā paccāgaccheyya[^419-8]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Tathā hi sā bhikkhave gāvī pabbateyyā paṇḍitā vyattā khettaññū kusalā visame pabbate carituṃ. Evam eva kho bhikkhave idh' ekacco bhikkhu paṇḍito vyatto khettaññū kusalo vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . .[^419-3] paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharituṃ[^419-9]; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . .[^419-3] dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan' ti; so dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno[^419-10] vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . .[^419-11] dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ pītiyā ca virāgā


<!-- PTS Page 420 -->
<a id="page-420"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 420]*

. . .[^420-1] tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno pītiyā ca virāgā . . .[^420-2] tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ sukhassa ca pahānā . . .[^420-1] catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so catutthaṃ jhānaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno sukhassa ca pahānā . . . pe[^420-3] . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tasa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā patighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^420-4] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā <<ananto ākāso>> ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā . . . pe[^420-5] . . . ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma <<anantaṃ viññāṇan>> ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma <<natthi kiñcī>> ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; so taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulī-


<!-- PTS Page 421 -->
<a id="page-421"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 421]*

karoti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, tassa evaṃ hoti ‘yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti; so saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ anabhihiṃsamāno sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati[^421-1].

3. Yato[^421-2] kho bhikkhave bhikkhu taṃ[^421-3] tad eva samāpattiṃ samāpajjati pi vuṭṭhāti pi, tassa mudu cittaṃ hoti kammaññaṃ, mudunā[^421-4] cittena[^421-4] kammaññena[^421-5] appamāṇo samādhi hoti subhāvito, so[^421-6] appamāṇena samādhinā subhāvitena yassa[^421-7] yassa[^421-7] abhiññā sacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhininnāmeti[^421-8] abhiññā sacchikiriyāya, tatra tatr' eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatane. So sace ākaṅkhati ‘anekavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ paccanubhaveyyaṃ: eko pi hutvā bahudhā assaṃ[^421-9] . . . pe[^421-10] . . . yāva Brahmalokā pi kāyena 'va saṃvatteyyan'; ti, tatra tatr'; eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatane. So[^421-11] sace ākaṅkhati ‘dibbāya sotadhātuyā . . . pe[^421-12] . . . sati sati āyatane. So sace ākaṅkhati ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajāneyyaṃ: sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittan ti pajāneyyaṃ . . . pe[^421-13] . . . vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ[^421-14] cittan[^421-14] ti[^421-14] pajāneyyaṃ[^421-14], avimuttaṃ[^421-15] vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittan ti pajāneyyan'; ti. tatra tatr'; eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatane. So sace ākaṅkhati ‘anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussareyyaṃ, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo . . . pe[^421-10] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussareyyan'; ti, tatra tatr'; eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatane.


<!-- PTS Page 422 -->
<a id="page-422"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 422]*

So sace āhaṅkhati ‘dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^422-1] . . . pe[^422-2] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajāneyyan'; ti, tatra tatr'; eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇati sati sati āyatane. So sace ākaṅkhati ‘āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^422-3] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyan'; ti, tatra tatr' eva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatane ti. XXXVI.

1. Paṭhamam p'; ahaṃ[^422-4] bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, dutiyam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, tatiyam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, catutthaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, ākāsānañcāyatanam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ[^422-5] p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi[^422-6] . . . pe[^422-3] . . . nevasaññānāsaññāyatanam[^422-5] p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi.

2. Paṭhamam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ[^422-7], kiñ c' etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ[^422-7]? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^422-8] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. So yad[^422-9] eva tattha hoti rūpagataṃ vedanāgataṃ saññāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viññāṇagataṃ, te dhamme aniccato dukkhato rogato gaṇḍato sallato aghato[^422-10] ābādhato parato palokato[^422-11]


<!-- PTS Page 423 -->
<a id="page-423"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 423]*

suññato anattato samanupassati. So tehi dhammehi cittaṃ paṭivāpeti[^423-1], so tehi dhammehi cittaṃ paṭivāpetvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati ‘etaṃ santaṃ etaṃ paṇītaṃ, yad idaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānan'; ti. So tattha ṭhito āsavānaṃ khayaṃ[^423-2] pāpuṇāti, no ce āsavānaṃ khayaṃ[^423-2] pāpuṇāti, ten'; eva dhammarāgena tāya dhammanandiyā pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave issāso vā issāsantevāsī vā tiṇapurisake[^423-3] vā mattikāpuñje[^423-4] vā yoggaṃ[^423-5] karitvā so aparena samayena dūre[^423-6] pāti[^423-7] ca hoti akkhaṇavedhi[^423-8] ca mahato ca kāyassa padāletā[^423-9]: evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^423-10] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; so yad eva tattha hoti rūpagataṃ vedanāgataṃ[^423-11] saññāgataṃ[^423-11] saṅkhāragataṃ viññāṇagataṃ, te dhamme aniccato dukkhato rogato gaṇḍato sallato aghato[^423-12] ābādhato parato palokato[^423-13] suññato anattato samanupassati; so tehi dhammehi cittaṃ paṭivāpeti, so tehi dhammehi cittaṃ paṭivāpetvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati ‘etaṃ santaṃ etaṃ paṇītaṃ, yad idaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānan'; ti; so tattha[^423-14] ṭhito āsavānaṃ khayaṃ[^423-2] pāpuṇāti, no ce āsavānaṃ khayaṃ[^423-2] pāpuṇāti, ten'; eva dhammarāgena tāya dhammanandiyā pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā.


<!-- PTS Page 424 -->
<a id="page-424"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 424]*

Paṭhamam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ[^424-1] vadāmī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

3. Dutiyam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya . . . pe[^424-2] . . . tatiyam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya . . . pe[^424-3] . . . catuttham p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya[^424-4] āsavānaṃ khayaṃ[^424-1] vādāmī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c' etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sukhassa[^424-5] ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubb'; eva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. So yad eva tattha hoti rūpagataṃ vedanāgataṃ saññāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viññāṇagataṃ, te dhamme aniccato dukkhato rogato gaṇḍato sallato aghato ābādhato parato palokato suññato anattato samanupassati. So tehi dhammehi cittaṃ paṭivāpeti[^424-6], so tehi dhammehi cittaṃ paṭivāpetvā[^424-6] amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati ‘etaṃ santaṃ etaṃ paṇītaṃ, yad idam sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānan'; ti. So tattha ṭhito āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, no ce āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, ten' eva dhammarāgena tāya dhammanandiyā pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave issāso vā issāsantevāsī vā tiṇapurisake vā mattikāpuñje vā yoggaṃ karitvā, so aparena samayena dūre pāti ca hoti akkhaṇavedhi ca mahato ca kāyassa padāletā[^424-6]: evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā . . . pe[^424-7] . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; so yad eva tattha hoti rūpagataṃ vedanāgataṃ[^424-8] . . . pe[^424-7] . . . anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā.


<!-- PTS Page 425 -->
<a id="page-425"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 425]*

Catuttham p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

4. Ākāsānañcāyatanam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave jhānaṃ nissāya āsāvānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c' etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^425-1] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^425-2] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. So yad eva tattha hoti vedanāgataṃ saññāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ . . .[^425-3] So . . .[^425-3] pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave issāso vā issāsantevāsī vā tiṇapurisake vā mattikāpuñje[^425-4] vā yoggaṃ karitvā so aparena samayena dūre pāti[^425-5] ca hoti akkhaṇavedhi ca mahato ca kāyassa padāletā: evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^425-1] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; so yad eva tattha hoti vedanāgataṃ saññāgataṃ[^425-6] . . . pe[^425-7] . . . anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā. Ākāsānañcāyatanam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ.

5. Viññāṇañcāyatanam p'; aham bhikkhave nissāya[^425-8] . . . pe[^425-7] . . . ākiñcaññāyatanam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī ti iti kho pan'; etaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ c'; etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ?

##### *[PTS Page 426]*

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. So yad eva tattha hoti vedanāgataṃ saññāgataṃ . . . pe[^426-1] . . . So . . .[^426-2] pañcannaṃ orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ tasmā lokā . . .[^426-1] Ākiñcaññāyatanam p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttaṃ. Iti kho bhikkhave yāvatā saññāsamāpatti, tāvatā aññāpaṭivedho[^426-3]. Yāni ca kho imāni bhikkhave[^426-4] āyatanāni: nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpatti ca saññāvedayitanirodho ca, jhāyī h'; ete[^426-5] bhikkhave bhikkhūhi samāpattikusalehi samāpattivuṭṭhānakusalehi[^426-6] samāpajjitvā vuṭṭhahitvā[^426-7] samakkhātabbānī[^426-8] ti vadāmī ti. XXXVII.

1. Evam[^426-9] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Ānando Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme. Tatra kho āyasmā Ānando bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Āvuso bhikkhavo[^426-10] ti. Āvuso ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Ānandassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Ānando etad avoca:--

2. Acchariyaṃ āvuso abbhutaṃ āvuso, yāvañ c'; idaṃ tena Bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhena sambādhe[^426-11] okāsādhigamo anubuddho sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ[^426-12] samatikkamāya[^426-13] dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya[^426-14] ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, tad eva nāma cakkhuṃ bhavissati, te rūpā[^426-15] tañ[^426-15]

<a id="page-427"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 427]*

cāyatanaṃ[^427-1] no[^427-2] paṭisaṃvedissati[^427-3]; tad eva nāma sotaṃ bhavissati, te saddā[^427-4] tañ[^427-4] cāyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedissati; tad eva nāma ghānaṃ bhavissati, te gandhā[^427-5] tañ[^427-5] cāyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedissati; sā ca[^427-6] nāma jivhā bhavissati, te rasā[^427-7] tañ[^427-7] cāyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedissati; so ca[^427-8] nāma kāyo bhavissati, te phoṭṭhabbā[^427-9] tañ[^427-9] cāyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedissatī ti.

3. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Udāyi[^427-10] āyasamantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avoca ‘saññī[^427-11]-m-eva nu kho āvuso Ānanda tad āyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedeti, udāhu asaññī[^427-12]'; ti? ‘Saññī[^427-11]-meva kho āvuso tad āyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedeti, no asaññī' ti. ‘Kiṃsaññī panāvuso tad āyatanaṃ no[^427-2] paṭisaṃvedetī'; ti?

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^427-13] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^427-14] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Evaṃsaññī pi kho āvuso tad āyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedeti.

5. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Evaṃsaññī pi kho āvuso tad āyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedeti.

6. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Evaṃsaññī pi kho āvuso tad āyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedeti.

7. Ekam idāhaṃ āvuso samayaṃ Sākete viharāmi Añjanavane Migadāye. Atha kho āvuso Jaṭilāgāhiyā[^427-15] bhik-

##### *[PTS Page 428]*

khunī yenāhaṃ ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṃ ṭhitā kho āvuso Jaṭilāgāhiyā[^428-1] bhikkhunī maṃ etad avoca ‘yāyaṃ[^428-2] bhante Ānanda samādhi na cābhinato[^428-3] na cāpanato[^428-4] na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritavato[^428-5] vimuttattā ṭhito ṭhitattā santusito[^428-6] santusitattā[^428-6] no paritassati, ayaṃ bhante Ānanda samādhi kimphalo[^428-7] vutto Bhagavatā'; ti? Evaṃ vutte ahaṃ[^428-8] āvuso Jaṭilāgāhiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ etad avocaṃ[^428-9] 'yāyaṃ[^428-10] bhagini samādhi na cābhinato na cāpanato na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritavato[^428-11] vimuttattā ṭhito ṭhitattā santusito[^428-6] santusitattā[^428-6] no paritassati, ayaṃ bhagini samādhi aññāphalo[^428-12] vutto Bhagavatā'; ti. Evaṃsaññī pi kho āvuso tad āyatanaṃ no paṭisaṃvedetī ti. XXXVIII.

1. Atha kho dve lokāyatikā brāhmaṇā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te {brāhmaṇā} Bhagavantaṃ etad avocuṃ:--

2. Pūraṇo bho[^428-13] Gotama Kassapo sabbaññū sabbadassāvī aparisesañāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti ‘carato ca me tiṭṭhato ca[^428-14] suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paccupaṭṭhitan'; ti. So evam āha ‘ahaṃ anantena ñāṇena antavantaṃ[^428-15] lokaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharāmī'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 429 -->
<a id="page-429"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 429]*

Ayam pi[^429-1] bho Gotama Nigaṇṭho[^429-2] Nāṭaputto[^429-3] sabbaññū sabbadassāvī aparisesaññāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti ‘carato ca me tiṭṭhato ca[^429-4] suttassa ca[^429-5] jāgarassa ca[^429-5] satataṃ[^429-6] samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paccupaṭṭhitan'; ti. So evam āha ‘ahaṃ antavantena[^429-7] ñāṇena antavantaṃ[^429-8] lokaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharāmī'; ti. Imesaṃ bho Gotama ubhinnaṃ ñāṇavādānaṃ ubhinnaṃ aññamaññaṃ vipaccanīkavādānaṃ[^429-9] ko saccaṃ āha[^429-10] ko musā ti?

3. Alaṃ brāhmaṇā, tiṭṭhat'; etaṃ: imesaṃ ubhinnaṃ ñāṇavādānaṃ ubhinnaṃ aññamaññaṃ vipaccanīkavādānaṃ ko saccaṃ āha[^429-10] ko musā ti[^429-10]. Dhammaṃ vo brāhmaṇā desissāmi, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bho'; ti kho te brāhmaṇā Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

4. Seyyathā pi brāhmaṇā cattāro purisā catuddisā ṭhitā paramāya[^429-11] gatiyā[^429-11] ca[^429-12] javena ca[^429-13] samannāgatā paramena ca padavītihārena, te[^429-10] evarūpena javena samannāgatā assu; seyyathā pi nāma daḷhadhammo dhanuggaho sikkhito katahattho katupāsano lahukena asanena appakasirena tiriyaṃ tālacchātiṃ[^429-14] atipāteyya, evarūpena ca padavītihārena; seyyathā pi nāma puratthimā samuddā pacchimo samuddo, atha puratthimāya[^429-15] disāya ṭhito puriso evaṃ vadeyya ‘ahaṃ gamanena lokassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī'; ti, so aññatr' eva asitapītakhāyitasāyitā[^429-16] aññatra[^429-17] uccārapassāvakammā aññatra[^429-17] niddākilamathapaṭivinodanā vassasatāyuko vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvā 'va lokassa antaṃ antarā

kālaṃ kareyya[^430-1], atha pacchimāya disāya . . . pe[^430-2] . . . atha[^430-3] uttarāya[^430-3] disāya[^430-3] . . .[^430-4]atha dakkhiṇāya disāya ṭhito puriso evaṃ vadeyya ‘ahaṃ gamanena lokassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī'; ti, so aññatr'; eva asitapītakhāyitasāyitā[^430-5] aññatra uccārapassāvakammā aññatra niddākilamathapaṭivinodanā vassasatāyuko vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvā 'va lokassa antaṃ antarā kālaṃ kareyya. Na cāhaṃ brāhmaṇā appatvā'; va lokassa antaṃ dukkhass'; antakiriyaṃ vadāmi[^430-9].

Ime kho brāhmaṇā pañca kāmaguṇā ariyassa vinaye loko ti vuccati[^430-10].
