11. Puna ca paraṃ brāhmaṇā bhikkhu sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, paññāya c'; assa[^431-8] disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā honti. Ayaṃ vuccati brāhmaṇā bhikkhu lo-


<!-- PTS Page 432 -->
<a id="page-432"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 432]*

kassa antam āgamma[^432-1] lokassa ante viharati tiṇṇo loke visattikan ti. XXXIX.

1. Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave devāsurasaṅgāmo[^432-2] samupabbūḷho[^432-3] ahosi. Tasmiṃ kho pana bhikkhave[^432-4] saṅgāme asurā jiniṃsu, devā parājiyiṃsu[^432-5]. Parājitā ca[^432-6] bhikkhave devā apayiṃsveva[^432-7], uttarenābhimukhā[^432-8] abhiyiṃsu[^432-9] asurā. Atha kho bhikkhave devānaṃ etad ahosi ‘abhiyant'[^432-10] eva kho asurā, yan nūna mayaṃ dutiyam[^432-11] pi asurehi saṅgāmeyyāmā'; ti.

2. Dutiyam pi kho bhikkhave devā asurehi saṅgāmesuṃ. Dutiyam pi kho bhikkhave asurā 'va[^432-12] jiniṃsu, devā parājiyiṃsu. Parājitā ca[^432-13] bhikkhave devā bhītā[^432-14] apayiṃsveva[^432-15], uttarenābhimukhā[^432-8] abhiyiṃsu asurā. Atha kho bhikkhave devānaṃ etad ahosi ‘abhiyant'; eva kho asurā, yan nūna mayaṃ tatiyam[^432-16] pi asurehi saṅgāmeyyāmā'; ti.

3. Tatiyam pi kho bhikkhave devā asurehi saṅgāmesuṃ. Tatiyam pi kho bhikkhave asurā 'va[^432-12] jiniṃsu, devā parājiyiṃsu. Parājitā ca bhikkhave devā[^432-17] bhītā devapuraṃ[^432-18] yeva pavisiṃsu. Devapuragatānañ ca pana[^432-19] bhikkhave devānaṃ etad ahosi ‘bhīruttānagatena[^432-20] kho dāni mayaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 433 -->
<a id="page-433"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 433]*

etarahi attanā viharāma akaraṇīyā asurehī'; ti. Asurānam pi bhikkhave etad ahosi ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dāni devā etarahi attanā viharanti akaraṇīyā[^433-1] amhehī[^433-2]'; ti.

4. Bhūtapubbaṃ bhikkhave devāsurasaṅgāmo samupabbūḷho[^433-3] ahosi. Tasmiṃ kho pana[^433-4] bhikkhave saṅgāme devā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiyiṃsu[^433-5]. Parājitā ca bhikkhave asurā apayiṃsveva[^433-6], dakkhiṇenābhimukhā[^433-7] abhiyiṃsu devā. Atha kho bhikkhave asurānaṃ etad ahosi ‘abhiyant'; eva kho devā, yan nūna mayaṃ dutiyam[^433-8] pi devehi saṅgāmeyyāmā'; ti.

5. Dutiyam pi kho bhikkhave asurā devehi saṅgāmesuṃ. Dutiyam pi kho bhikkhave devā 'va[^433-9] jiniṃsu, asurā parājiyiṃsu. Parājitā ca[^433-10] bhikkhave asurā apayiṃsveva[^433-11], dakkhiṇenābhimukhā[^433-7] abhiyiṃsu devā. Atha kho bhikkhave asurānaṃ etad ahosi ‘abhiyant'; eva kho devā, yan nūna mayaṃ tatiyam[^433-12] pi devehi saṅgāmeyyāmā'; ti.

6. Tatiyam[^433-12] pi kho bhikkhave asurā devehi saṅgāmesuṃ. Tatiyam pi kho bhikkhave devā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiyiṃsu. Parājitā ca[^433-10] bhikkhave asurā bhītā asurapuraṃ[^433-13] yeva pavisiṃsu, asurapuragatānañ ca pana[^433-14] bhikkhave asurānaṃ etad ahosi ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dāni[^433-15] mayaṃ etarahi attanā viharāma akaraṇīyā devehī'; ti. Devānam pi bhikkhave etad ahosi ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dāni asurā etarahi attanā viharanti akaraṇīyā amhehī'; ti.

7. Evam eva kho bhikkhave yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . . pe[^433-16] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye bhikkhussa evaṃ hoti ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dānāhaṃ[^433-17] etarahi attanā


<!-- PTS Page 434 -->
<a id="page-434"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 434]*

viharāmi akaraṇīyo Mārassā'; ti, Mārassāpi bhikkhave pāpimato evaṃ hoti ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dāni bhikkhu etarahi attanā viharati akaraṇīyo mayhan'; ti.

8. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye bhikkhu vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^434-1] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye bhikkhussa evaṃ hoti ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dānāhaṃ etarahi attanā viharāmi akaraṇīyo Mārassā' ti, Mārassāpi bhikkhave pāpimato evaṃ hoti ‘bhīruttānagatena kho dāni bhikkhu etarahi attanā viharati akaraṇīyo mayhan'; ti.

9. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^434-2] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^434-3] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu antam akāsi Māraṃ[^434-4] apadaṃ[^434-5] vadhitvā[^434-6] Māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gato pāpimato[^434-7].

10. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye bhikkhu sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . .[^434-8] sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . .[^434-8] sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . .[^434-8] sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, paññāya c'; assa[^434-9] disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā honti: ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu antam akāsi Māraṃ apadaṃ[^434-10] vadhitvā[^434-11] Māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gato pāpimato tiṇṇo loke visattikan ti.


<!-- PTS Page 435 -->
<a id="page-435"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 435]*

XL.

1. Yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye āraññakassa[^435-1] nāgassa gocarapasutassa hatthī pi hatthiniyo pi hatthikalabhā[^435-2] pi hatthicchāpā pi purato purato gantvā tiṇaggāṇi chindanti, tena bhikkhave āraññako nāgo aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati; yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye āraññakassa nāgassa gocarapasutassa hatthī pi hatthiniyo pi hatthikalabhā[^435-3] pi[^435-3] hatthicchāpā[^435-3] pi[^435-3] obhaggobhaggaṃ sākhābhaṅgaṃ[^435-4] khādanti, tena bhikkhave āraññako nāgo aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati; yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye āraññakassa nāgassa ogāhaṃ otiṇṇassa hatthī pi hatthiniyo pi hatthikalabhā pi hatthicchāpā pi purato purato gantvā soṇḍāya udakaṃ āloḷenti[^435-5], tena bhikkhave āraññako nāgo aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati; yasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye āraññakassa nāgassa ogāhaṃ[^435-6] otiṇṇassa[^435-7] hatthiniyo kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyo[^435-8] gacchanti, tena bhikkhave āraññako nāgo aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati.

2. Tasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye āraññakassa nāgassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho etarahi ākiṇṇo[^435-9] viharāmi hatthīhi hatthinīhi hatthikalabhehi[^435-10] hatthicchāpehi[^435-11], chinnaggāni c'; eva tiṇāni khādāmi, obhaggobhaggañ ca me sākhābhaṅgaṃ[^435-12] khādanti[^435-13], āvilāni[^435-14] ca pānīyāni pivāmi, ogāhañ[^435-15] ca me otiṇṇassa[^435-16] hatthiniyo kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyo[^435-8] gacchanti; yan nūnāhaṃ eko gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭho vihareyyan'; ti. So aparena samayena eko gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭho viharati, acchinnaggāni c'; eva[^435-17] tiṇāni khādati, obhaggobhaggañ c'; assa[^435-18]


<!-- PTS Page 436 -->
<a id="page-436"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 436]*

sākhābhaṅgaṃ[^436-1] na[^436-2] khādanti[^436-3], anāvilāni ca[^436-4] pānīyāni pivati, ogāhañ[^436-5] c'; assa[^436-6] otiṇṇassa[^436-7] na[^436-8] hatthiniyo kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyo[^436-9] gacchanti. Tasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye āraññakassa nāgassa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho pubbe ākiṇṇo[^436-10] vihāsiṃ[^436-11] hatthīhi hatthinīhi hatthikalabhehi hatthicchāpehi, chinnaggāni c'; eva tiṇāni khādiṃ[^436-12], obhaggobhaggañ ca me sākhābhaṅgaṃ[^436-13] khādiṃsu[^436-14], āvilāni ca pānīyāni apāyiṃ[^436-15], ogāhañ[^436-16] ca me otiṇṇassa[^436-17] hatthiniyo kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyo[^436-18] agamaṃsu; so 'haṃ[^436-19] etarahi eko gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭho viharāmi, acchinnaggāni c'; eva tiṇāni khādāmi, obhaggobhaggañ ca me sākhābhaṅgaṃ na[^436-20] khādanti[^436-14], anāvilāni ca pānīyāni pivāmi, ogāhañ[^436-5] ca me[^436-21] otiṇṇassa[^436-22] na hatthiniyo kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyo[^436-23] gacchantī'; ti. So soṇḍāya sākhābhaṅgaṃ[^436-13] bhañjitvā sākhābhaṅgena kāyaṃ parimadditvā[^436-24] attamano kaṇḍuṃ[^436-25] saṃhanti[^436-26].

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu ākiṇṇo[^436-10] viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakehi upāsikāhi raññā rājamahāmattehi titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi, tasmiṃ bhikkhave samaye bhikkhussa evaṃ hoti ‘ahaṃ kho etarahi ākiṇṇo viharāmi bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakehi upāsikāhi raññā rājamahāmattehi titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi, yan nūnāhaṃ eko gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭho vihareyyan'; ti. So vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ bhajati araññaṃ[^436-27]


<!-- PTS Page 437 -->
<a id="page-437"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 437]*

rukkhamūlaṃ[^437-1] pabbataṃ kandaraṃ[^437-2] giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ[^437-3] abbhokāsaṃ palālapuñjaṃ, so araññagato[^437-4] vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā[^437-5] ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā[^437-6]; so abhijjhaṃ loke pahāya vigatābhijjhena cetasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisodheti; vyāpādapadosaṃ[^437-7] pahāya avyāpannacitto viharati, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī vyāpādapadosā cittaṃ parisodheti; thīnamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathīnamiddho viharati, ālokasaññī sato sampajāno thīnamiddhā cittaṃ parisodheti; uddhaccakukkuccaṃ pahāya anuddhato viharati, ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacitto uddhaccakukkuccā cittaṃ parisodheti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikiccho viharati, akathaṃkathī kusalesu dhammesu vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisodheti. So ime pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso[^437-8] upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe vivicc' eva kāmehi . . . pe[^437-9] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, so attamano kaṇḍuṃ[^437-10] saṃhanti[^437-11]; vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^437-12] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, so attamano kaṇḍuṃ saṃhanti; sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^437-13] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^437-14] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati, so attamano kaṇḍuṃ saṃhanti; sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . .[^437-15] sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī' ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . . sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . .[^437-16] sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññā-


<!-- PTS Page 438 -->
<a id="page-438"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 438]*

yatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, paññāya c'; assa[^438-1] disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā honti, so attamano kaṇḍuṃ saṃhantī ti. XLI.

1. Evam[^438-2] me sutaṃ. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Mallesu[^438-3] viharati Uruvelokappaṃ nāma Mallānaṃ[^438-4] nigamo. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Uruvelakappaṃ[^438-5] piṇḍāya pāvisi. Uruvelakappe piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ āmantesi ‘idh'; eva tāva tvaṃ Ānanda hohi, yāvāhaṃ[^438-6] Mahāvanaṃ ajjhogāhāmi divāvihārāyā'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paccassosi. Atha kho Bhagavā Mahāvanaṃ ajjhogāhetvā aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle divāvihāraṃ nisīdi.

2. Atha kho Tapusso[^438-7] gahapati yenāyasmā Ānando ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Tapusso gahapati āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avoca ‘mayaṃ bhante Ānanda gihī kāmabhogī[^438-8] kāmārāmā[^438-9] kāmaratā kāmasammuditā[^438-10], tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ gihīnaṃ kāmabhogīnaṃ kāmārāmānaṃ kāmaratānaṃ kāmasammuditānaṃ papāto viya khāyati yad idaṃ nekkhammaṃ[^438-11]; sutam me[^438-12] taṃ[^438-12] bhante: imasmiṃ dhammavinaye daharānaṃ daharānaṃ[^438-13] bhikkhūnaṃ nekkhamme[^438-14] cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati <<etaṃ santan>> ti passato; tayidaṃ bhante imasmiṃ dhammavinaye bhikkhūnaṃ bahunā janena visabhāgo[^438-15] yad idaṃ nekkhamman[^438-16]'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 439 -->
<a id="page-439"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 439]*

‘Atthi kho etaṃ gahapati kathāpābhataṃ[^439-1], Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya āyāma gahapati, yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamissāma, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavato etam atthaṃ ārocessāma[^439-2]; yathā no Bhagavā vyākarissati[^439-3], tathā tam[^439-4] dhāressāmā[^439-4]'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho Tapusso gahapati āyasmato Ānandassa paccassosi.

3. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando Tapussena gahapatinā saddhiṃ yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ . . . pe[^439-5] . . . Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ayaṃ bhante Tapusso gahapati evam āha: mayaṃ bhante Ānanda gihī kāmabhogī kāmārāmā kāmaratā kāmasammuditā[^439-6], tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ gihīnaṃ kāmabhogīnaṃ kāmārāmānaṃ kāmaratānaṃ kāmasammuditānaṃ[^439-6] papāto[^439-7] viya khāyati yad idaṃ nekkhammaṃ; sutam me[^439-8] taṃ[^439-8] bhante: imasmiṃ dhammavinaye daharānaṃ daharānaṃ[^439-9] bhikkhūnaṃ nekkhamme cittaṃ pakkhandati[^439-10] pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati <<etaṃ santan>> ti passato; tayidaṃ bhante imasmiṃ dhammavinaye bhikkhūnaṃ bahunā janena visabhāgo yad idaṃ nekkhamman'; ti.

4. Evam etaṃ Ānanda evam[^439-11] etaṃ Ānanda, mayham pi kho Ānanda pubb'; eva sambodhā anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattass'; eva sato etad ahosi: sādhu nekkhammaṃ sādhu pavineko ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda nekkhamme cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me nekkhamme cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: kāmesu kho[^439-12] me[^439-12] ādīnavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, nekkhamme[^439-13] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so ca me[^439-14]


<!-- PTS Page 440 -->
<a id="page-440"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 440]*

anāsevito[^440-1]; tasmā me[^440-2] nekkhamme cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: sace kho ahaṃ kāmesu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^440-3] bahulīkareyyaṃ[^440-4], nekkhamme ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma[^440-5] tam āseveyyaṃ; ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me nekkhamme cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena kāmesu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^440-6] bahulam[^440-6] akāsiṃ[^440-7], nekkhamme[^440-8] ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseviṃ[^440-9]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda nekkhamme cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati ‘etam santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena[^440-10] samayena[^440-10] vivicc'; eva kāmehi . . .[^440-11] paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharāmi. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda iminā vihārena viharato kāmasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa[^440-12] me hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi Ānanda sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-deva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa[^440-13] me kāmasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho.

5. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: yan nūnāhaṃ vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^440-14] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda avitakke cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me avitakke cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: vitakkesu[^440-15] kho me ādīnavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, avitakke[^440-16] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so

So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena vitakkesu[^441-4] ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^441-5] bahulam[^441-5] akāsiṃ[^441-6], avitakke ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma[^441-7] tam āseviṃ[^441-8]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda avitakke cittaṃ pakkhandati[^441-9] pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena[^441-10] samayena[^441-10] vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^441-11] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharāmi. Seyyathā pi Ānanda sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa[^441-12] me[^441-13] vitakkasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho.

6. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: yan nūnāhaṃ pītiyā ca virāgā . . .[^441-14] tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda nippītike cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi ‘ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me nippītike cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: pītiyā kho me ādīnavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, nippītike[^441-15] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so ca me anāsevito; tasmā me nippītike cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na san-

So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena pītiyā ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^442-3] bahulam[^442-3] akāsiṃ[^442-4], nippītike[^442-5] ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseviṃ[^442-6]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda adukkhamasukhe cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me adukkhamasukhe cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? <a id="page-443"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 443]*

taṃ[^443-1] bahulīkareyyaṃ[^443-1], adukkhamasukhe ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseveyyaṃ; ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me adukkhamasukhe cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena upekhāsukhe ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^443-2] bahulam[^443-2] akāsiṃ, adukkhamasukhe ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseviṃ[^443-3]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda adukkhamasukhe cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena sukhassa ca pahānā[^443-4] . . . pe[^443-5] . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharāmi. Tassa mayham Ānanda iminā vihārena viharato upekhāsahagatā[^443-6] saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi Ānanda sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa me upekhāsahagatā[^443-7] saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho.

8. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^443-8] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^443-9] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso' ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda ākāsānañcāyatane cittaṃ na[^443-10] pakkhandati na[^443-10] ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me ākāsānañcāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: rūpesu[^443-11] kho me ādīnavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, ākāsānañcāyatane[^443-12] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so ca me anāsevito; tasmā[^443-13] me ākāsānañcāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'

##### *[PTS Page 444]*

ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: sace kho ahaṃ rūpesu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^444-1] bahulīkareyyaṃ[^444-1], ākāsānañcāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma[^444-2] tam āseveyyaṃ[^444-3]; ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me ākāsānañcāyatane cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena rūpesu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^444-1] bahulam[^444-1] akāsiṃ[^444-4], ākāsānañcāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma[^444-5] tam āseviṃ[^444-6]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda ākāsanañcāyatane cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena[^444-7] samayena[^444-7] sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharāmi. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda iminā vihārena viharato rūpasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho. Seyyathā pi Ānanda sukhino dukkhaṃ uppajjeyya yāva-d-eva ābādhāya, evam ev'; assa me rūpasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho.

9. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda viññāṇañcāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me viññāṇañcāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ākāsānañcāyatane kho me ādīnavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, viññāṇañcāyatane[^444-8] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so ca me anāsevito; tasmā me viññāṇañcāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: sace kho ahaṃ ākāsānañcāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^444-1]

bahulīkareyyaṃ[^445-1], viññāṇañcāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseveyyaṃ; ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me viññāṇañcāyatane cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena ākāsānañcāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^445-1] bahulam[^445-1] akāsiṃ[^445-2], viññāṇañcāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseviṃ[^445-3]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda viññāṇañcāyatane cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena[^445-4] samayena[^445-4] sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharāmi. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda iminā vihārena viharato ākāsānañcāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho. 10. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī' ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: viññāṇañcāyatane kho me ādinavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, ākiñcaññāyatane[^445-5] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so ca me anāsevito; tasmā me ākiñcaññāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan' ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: sace kho ahaṃ viññāṇañcāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^445-6] bahulīkareyyaṃ[^445-6], ākiñcaññāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseveyyaṃ[^445-7]; ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me ākiñcaññā-

yatane cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena[^446-3] samayena[^446-3] sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharāmi. 11. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: yan nūnāhaṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja vihareyyan ti. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda nevasaññānāsaññāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo yena me nevasaññānāsaññāyatane cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato? Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: sace kho ahaṃ ākiñcaññāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^446-1] bahulīkareyyaṃ[^446-1], nevasaññānāsaññāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseveyyaṃ[^446-5]; ṭhānaṃ kho pan'; etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me nevasaññānāsaññāyatane cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato.

So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena ākiñcaññāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^447-1] bahulam[^447-1] akāsiṃ[^447-2], nevasaññānāsaññāyatane ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseviṃ[^447-3]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda iminā vihārena viharato ākiñcaññāyatanasahagatā saññāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svāssa me hoti ābādho. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: nevasaññānāsaññāyatane kho me ādīnavo adiṭṭho, so ca me abahulīkato, saññāvedayitanirodhe[^447-5] ānisaṃso anadhigato, so ca me anāsevito; tasmā me saññāvedayitanirodhe cittaṃ na pakkhandati na ppasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda etad ahosi: sace kho ahaṃ nevasaññānāsaññāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^447-6] bahulīkareyyaṃ[^447-6], saññāvedayitanirodhe ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseveyyaṃ; ṭhānaṃ kho pan' etaṃ vijjati, yaṃ me saññāvedayitanirodhe cittaṃ pakkhandeyya pasīdeyya santiṭṭheyya vimucceyya ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passato. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena samayena nevasaññānāsaññāyatane ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ[^447-1] bahulam[^447-1] akā-

siṃ[^448-1], saññāvedayitanirodhe ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tam āseviṃ[^448-2]. Tassa mayhaṃ Ānanda saññāvedayitanirodhe cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuccati ‘etaṃ santan'; ti passati. So kho ahaṃ Ānanda aparena[^448-3] samayena[^448-3] sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharāmi, paññāya ca me disvā āsavā parikkhayaṃ agamaṃsu.

13. Yāvakīvañ cāhaṃ Ānanda imā nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyo na[^448-4] evaṃ anulomapaṭilomaṃ samāpajjim[^448-5] pi[^448-6] vuṭṭhahiṃ[^448-7] pi[^448-8], neva tāvāhaṃ Ānanda sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambuddho[^448-9] paccaññāsiṃ. Yato[^448-10] ca kho ahaṃ Ānanda imā nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyo evaṃ anulomapaṭilomaṃ samāpajjim[^448-11] pi[^448-12] vuṭṭhahim[^448-7] pi[^448-12], athāhaṃ Ānanda sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambuddho paccaññāsiṃ. Ñāṇañ ca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi ‘akuppā me cetovimutti[^448-13], ayam antimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavo'; ti. Mahāvaggo[^448-14] catuttho. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^448-15]: Dve ca[^448-16] vihārā[^448-17] nibbānaṃ gāvī[^448-18] jhānena pañcamaṃ Ānando brāhmaṇā[^448-19] devā[^448-20] nāgena[^448-21] Tapussena[^448-22] cā ti.

##### *[PTS Page 449]*

XLII[^449-1].

1. Evaṃ[^449-2] me[^449-2] sutaṃ[^449-2]. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Ānando Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Udāyi[^449-3] yenāyasmā Ānando ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^449-4] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Udāyi āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avoca ‘vuttam idaṃ āvuso Pañcālacaṇḍena devaputtena: Sambādhe[^449-5] gataṃ[^449-6] okāsaṃ avidā[^449-7] bhūrimedhaso[^449-8] yo[^449-9] jhānam abujjhi[^449-10] buddho pātilīnanisabho munī ti. Katamo nu[^449-2] kho[^449-2] āvuso sambādho, katamo sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā'; ti?

2. Pañc'; ime āvuso kāmaguṇā sambādho, vutto[^449-11] Bhagavatā. Katame pañca? Cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyārūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sotaviññeyyā saddā . . .[^449-12] ghānaviññeyyā gandhā . . . jivhāviññeyyā rasā . . . kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā. Ime kho āvuso pañca kāmaguṇā sambādho vutto[^449-11] Bhagavatā.

3. Idhāvuso bhikkhu vivicc'; ev kāmehi . . . pe[^449-13] . . . paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha[^449-14] p'; atthi[^449-15] sambādho, kiñ ca tattha sambādho?


<!-- PTS Page 450 -->
<a id="page-450"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 450]*

Yad[^450-1] eva[^450-1] tattha vitakkavicārā aniruddhā[^450-2] honti, ayam ettha sambādho.

4. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . . pe[^450-3] . . . dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha p'; atthi sambādho, kiñ[^450-4] ca[^450-4] tattha sambādho? Yad eva tattha pīti aniruddhā hoti, ayam ettha sambādho

5. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā . . . pe[^450-5] . . . tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha p'; atthi sambādho, kiñ[^450-4] ca[^450-4] tattha sambādho? Yad eva tattha upekhāsukhaṃ aniruddhaṃ hoti, ayam ettha sambādho.

6. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā . . . pe[^450-6] . . . catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha p'; atthi sambādho, kiñ[^450-4] ca[^450-4] tattha sambādho? Yad eva tattha rūpasaññā aniruddhā hoti, ayam ettha sambādho.

7. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^450-7] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^450-8] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha p'; atthi sambādho, kiñ ca tattha sambādho? Yad eva tattha ākāsānañcāyatanasaññā aniruddhā hoti, ayam ettha sambādho.

8. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anataṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcā-

yatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha p'; atthi sambādho, kiñ ca tattha sambādho? Yad eva tattha viññāṇañcāyatanasaññā aniruddhā hoti, ayam ettha sambādho.

9. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. 10. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto Bhagavatā pariyāyena. Tattha p'; atthi sambādho, kiñ ca tattha sambādho? Yad eva tattha nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññā aniruddhā hoti, ayam ettha sambādho.

11. Puna ca paraṃ āvuso bhikkhu sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, paññāya c'; assa[^451-1] disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā honti. Ettāvatā pi kho āvuso sambādhe okāsādhigamo vutto[^451-2] Bhagavatā nippariyāyenā ti. XLIII.

Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^457-1]: Khemo ca amataṃ c'; eva abhayaṃ[^457-2] passaddhiyena ca Nirodho anupubbo c'; eva dhammaṃ pahāya bhabbena cā ti. LXIII[^457-3].

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati, ātāpi sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ; vedanāsu vedanānupassī[^457-6] viharati[^457-6] . . . pe[^457-7] . . . citte cittānupassī[^457-6] viharati[^457-6] . . . dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati, ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ. LXX.

1. Pañc'; imāni bhikkhave uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni. Katamāni pañca.

2. Rūparāgo, arūparāgo, māno, uddhaccaṃ, avijjā. Imāni kho bhikkhave pañc'; uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni.

3. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānaṃ pahānāya . . .[^460-1] ime cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvetabbā ti. LXXI.

1. Pañc'; ime bhikkhave cetokhilā. Katame pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na[^460-2] namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya. Yassa[^460-3] cittaṃ na[^460-2] namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamo cetokhilo.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu dhamme kaṅkhati . . . pe[^460-4] . . . saṅghe kaṅkhati . . .[^460-5] sikkhāya kaṅkhati . . .[^460-5] sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti anattamano āhatacitto[^460-6] khilajāto[^460-7]. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti anattamano āhatacitto[^460-6] khilajāto, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya. Yassa[^460-8] cittaṃ na[^460-2] namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañcamo cetokhilo. Ime[^460-9] kho bhikkhave pañca cetokhilā.

4. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ cetokhilānaṃ pahānāya . . .[^460-4] ime cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvetabbā ti.

##### *[PTS Page 461]*

LXXII.

1. Pañc'; ime bhikkhave cetaso vinibandhā. Katame pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu avītarāgo hoti avītacchando[^461-1] avītapemo avītapipāso avītapariḷāho avītataṇho. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu avītarāgo hoti avītacchando avītapemo avītapipāso avītapariḷāho avītataṇho, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya. Yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamo cetaso vinibandho.

3. Puna ca paraṃ bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye avītarāgo hoti . . . rūpe avītarāgo hoti . . . yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ[^461-2] middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharati . . . aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti. Yo so bhikkhave bhikkhu aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati ‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā'; ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya. Yassa[^461-3] cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyogāya sātacchāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañcamo cetaso vinibandho. Ime kho bhikkhave pañca cetaso vinibandhā.

4. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ cetaso vinibandhānaṃ pahānāya . . .[^461-4] ime cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvetabbā ti. Satipaṭṭhānavaggo[^461-5] sattamo[^461-5]. Tatr'; uddānaṃ[^461-6]:

Sikkhā nīvaraṇā kāmā khandhā ca[^462-1] orambhāgiyā Gati maccheraṃ[^462-2] c'; eva[^462-3] uddhambhāgiyā[^462-4] aṭṭhamaṃ Cetokhila-vinibandhā[^462-5] ti. LXXIII-LXXXI[^462-6].

1. Pañc'; imāni bhikkhave sikkhādubbalyāni. Katamāni pañca?

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya ime cattāro sammappadhānā bhāvetabbā. (Yāva[^462-9] vaggā[^462-9] sammappadhānavasena vitthārenti[^462-10].)

1. Pañc'; ime bhikkhave cetaso vinibandhā. Katame pañca?

3. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ cetaso vinibandhānaṃ pahānāya cattāro sammappadhānā bhāvetabbā. Katame cattāro?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya . . .[^463-2] anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya . . . uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ cetaso vinibandhānaṃ pahānāya ime cattāro sammappadhānā bhāvetabbā ti. Sammappadhānavaggo[^463-3] aṭṭhamo[^463-3]. LXXXIII-XCI[^463-4].

1. Pañc'; imāni bhikkhave sikkhādubbalyāni. Katamāni pañca?

2. Pāṇātipāto . . . pe[^463-1] . . . surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānaṃ. Imāni kho bhikkhave pañca sikkhādubbalyāni.

3. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya cattāro iddhipādā bhāvetabbā. Katame cattāro?


<!-- PTS Page 464 -->
<a id="page-464"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 464]*

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti, viriyasamādhi- . . .[^464-1]cittasamādhi- . . .[^464-1]vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya ime cattāro iddhipādā bhāvetabbā ti. (Sesaṃ[^464-2] iddhipādavasena vitthāretabbaṃ[^464-3].) XCII.

1. Pañc'; ime bhikkhave cetaso vinibandhā. Katame pañca?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāmesu avītarāgo hoti . . . pe[^464-4] . . . Ime kho bhikkhave pañca cetaso vinibandhā.

3. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ cetaso vinibandhānaṃ pahānāya ime cattāro iddhipādā bhāvetabbā. Katame cattāro?

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti, viriyasamādhi- . . . cittasamādhi- . . . vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannaṃ cetaso vinibandhānaṃ pahānāya ime cattāro iddhipādā bhāvetabbā ti. Iddhipādavaggo[^464-5] navamo[^464-5]. Cattāro satipaṭṭhānā padhānā[^464-6] caturo pade[^464-7] Cattāro iddhipādā[^464-8] pi[^464-8] purimehi ca[^464-9] yojaye ti[^464-10].


<!-- PTS Page 465 -->
<a id="page-465"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 465]*

XCIII[^465-1].

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya nava dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame nava?

2. Asubhasaññā, maraṇasaññā, āhāre paṭikūlasaññā[^465-2], sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā[^465-3], aniccasaññā, anicce dukkhasaññā, dukkhe anattasaññā, pahānasaññā, virāgasaññā. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime nava dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^465-4]. XCIV.

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya nava dhammā bhāvetabbā. Katame nava? Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ, ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ, viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ, ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ, nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ, saññāvedayitanirodho. Rāgassa bhikkhave abhiññāya ime nava dhammā bhāvetabbā ti[^465-4]. XCV-C.

1. Rāgassa bhikkhave pariññāya . . .[^465-5] parikkhāya . . .[^465-5] pahānāya . . .[^465-5]khayāya . . .[^465-5]vayāya . . .[^465-5]virāgāya . . .[^465-5] nirodhāya . . .[^465-5] cāgāya . . .[^465-5]paṭinissaggāya ime nava dhammā bhāvetabbā.

2. Dosassa . . . mohassa kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa palāsassa issāya[^465-6] macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa[^465-7] thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa abhiññāya . . . pariññāya . . . parikkhayāya . . . pahānāya . . . khayāya . . . vayāya . . . virāgāya . . .


<!-- PTS Page 466 -->
<a id="page-466"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 466]*

nirodhāya . . . cāgāya . . . paṭinissaggāya ime nava dhammā bhāvetabbā ti. Idam[^466-1] avoca Bhagavā. Attamanā te bhikkhū Bhagavato bhāsitaṃ abhinandun ti. Navakanipātaṃ[^466-2] samattaṃ[^466-2].

Iminā likkhitapuññena bhavābhave saṃsaranto ucce kule su-ukkaṭṭhasandhiṃ labhitvāna jātakālato paṭṭhāya pariyattisāsane ekapadakkharaṃ disvā sabbaṃ nisesaṃ jānitvā dhāreyyuṃ. Nibbānapaccayo hotu. Then follows the year in Burmese. --M8 adds: Idaṃ me puññaṃ āsavakkhayāvahaṃ hotu, then some words in Burmese, after which nibbānapaccayo hotu, then again some words in Burmese.
